from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
import os
import json
import logging
import psutil
import gc
import pynvml
from datetime import datetime

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

pynvml.nvmlInit()

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.device_ids = device_ids or list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.model = None
        self.tokenizer = None
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.current_device_idx = 0
    
    def load(self):
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")
            
            # Load tokenizer with local caching
            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True
            )
            
            # Configure device mapping
            device_map = {
                \\\"model.embed_tokens\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.0\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.1\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.2\\\": 0,

                \\\"model.layers.23\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.24\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.25\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.26\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.27\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.28\\\": 0,

                \\\"model.layers.60\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.61\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.norm\\\": 1,
                \\\"lm_head\\\": 1
            }
            logger.info(f\\\"Using manual device map\\\")
            
            # Load model with optimizations
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                device_map=device_map,
                torch_dtype=torch.float16,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                low_cpu_mem_usage=True
            )
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

# Initialize models dictionary
models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {}

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
    logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")
    
    # Initialize model
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    # Load models
    for name, model in models.items():
        if not model.load():
            logger.error(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")
            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\", \\\"models\\\": list(models.keys())}

class ModelConfig:

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
    logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")
    
    # Initialize model
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    # Load models
    for name, model in models.items():
        if not model.load():
            logger.error(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")
            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")

async def health_check():

class ModelConfig:

    prompt: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    agent_id: str

    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path

            # Load tokenizer with local caching
            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                use_fast=False
            )
            
            # Configure device mapping
            device_map = {
                \\\"model.embed_tokens\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.0\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.1\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.2\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.3\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.4\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.5\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.6\\\": 0,

                \\\"model.layers.60\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.61\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.norm\\\": 1,
                \\\"lm_head\\\": 1
            }
            logger.info(f\\\"Using manual device map\\\")
            
            # Load model with optimizations
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                device_map=device_map,
                torch_dtype=torch.float32,  # Full precision
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                low_cpu_mem_usage=True
            )
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7) -> str:
        try:
            inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\").to(\\\"cuda\\\")
            
            with torch.no_grad():
                outputs = self.model.generate(
                    **inputs,
                    max_length=max_length,
                    temperature=temperature,
                    do_sample=True,
                    pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id
                )
            
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
            self.total_requests += 1
            self.total_tokens += len(outputs[0])
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            return response
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

# Initialize models dictionary
models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {}

# Authorized agents for initial testing
AUTHORIZED_AGENTS = set([f\\\"agent_{i}\\\" for i in range(1, 51)])  # First 50 agents

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
    logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")
    
    # Initialize multiple model instances
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1-1\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1-2\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1-3\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    # Load models
    for name, model in models.items():
        if not model.load():
            logger.error(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")
            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\", \\\"models\\\": list(models.keys())}

@app.post(\\\"/generate\\\")
async def generate(request: GenerateRequest):
    # Check agent authorization
    if request.agent_id not in AUTHORIZED_AGENTS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\\\"Agent not authorized\\\")
    
    # Simple round-robin load balancing
    model_names = list(models.keys())
    selected_model = models[random.choice(model_names)]
    
    try:
        response = selected_model.generate(
            prompt=request.prompt,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        return {\\\"response\\\": response}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
import os
import json
import logging
import psutil
import gc
import pynvml
from datetime import datetime
import random

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

pynvml.nvmlInit()

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class GenerateRequest(BaseModel):
    prompt: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    agent_id: str

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.device_ids = device_ids or list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.model = None
        self.tokenizer = None
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.current_device_idx = 0
    
    def load(self):
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")
            
            # Load tokenizer with local caching
            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                use_fast=False
            )
            
            # Configure device mapping
            device_map = {
                \\\"model.embed_tokens\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.0\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.1\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.2\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.3\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.4\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.5\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.6\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.7\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.8\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.9\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.10\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.11\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.12\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.13\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.14\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.15\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.16\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.17\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.18\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.19\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.20\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.21\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.22\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.23\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.24\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.25\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.26\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.27\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.28\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.29\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.30\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.31\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.32\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.33\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.34\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.35\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.36\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.37\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.38\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.39\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.40\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.41\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.42\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.43\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.44\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.45\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.46\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.47\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.48\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.49\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.50\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.51\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.52\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.53\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.54\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.55\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.56\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.57\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.58\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.59\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.60\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.61\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.norm\\\": 1,
                \\\"lm_head\\\": 1
            }
            logger.info(f\\\"Using manual device map\\\")
            
            # Load model with optimizations
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                device_map=device_map,
                torch_dtype=torch.float32,  # Full precision
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                low_cpu_mem_usage=True
            )
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7) -> str:
        try:
            # Move model to CUDA if not already
            if not next(self.model.parameters()).is_cuda:
                self.model.cuda()
            
            # Tokenize and move to CUDA
            inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")
            inputs = {k: v.cuda() for k, v in inputs.items()}
            
            with torch.no_grad():
                outputs = self.model.generate(
                    **inputs,
                    max_length=max_length,
                    temperature=temperature,
                    do_sample=True,
                    pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id
                )
            
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
            self.total_requests += 1
            self.total_tokens += len(outputs[0])
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            return response
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

    logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")
    
    # Initialize multiple model instances
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1-1\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1-2\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1-3\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    # Load models
    for name, model in models.items():
        if not model.load():
            logger.error(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")
            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\", \\\"models\\\": list(models.keys())}

@app.post(\\\"/generate\\\")
async def generate(request: GenerateRequest):
    # Check agent authorization
    if request.agent_id not in AUTHORIZED_AGENTS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\\\"Agent not authorized\\\")
    
    # Simple round-robin load balancing
    model_names = list(models.keys())
    selected_model = models[random.choice(model_names)]
    
    try:
        response = selected_model.generate(
            prompt=request.prompt,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        return {\\\"response\\\": response}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer, PreTrainedTokenizer
import os
import json
import logging
import psutil
import gc
import pynvml
from datetime import datetime
import random

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

pynvml.nvmlInit()

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class GenerateRequest(BaseModel):
    prompt: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    agent_id: str

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.device_ids = device_ids or list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.model = None
        self.tokenizer = None
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.current_device_idx = 0
    
    def load(self):
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")
            
            # Configure device mapping
            device_map = {
                \\\"model.embed_tokens\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.0\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.1\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.2\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.3\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.4\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.5\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.6\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.7\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.8\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.9\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.10\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.11\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.12\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.13\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.14\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.15\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.16\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.17\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.18\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.19\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.20\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.21\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.22\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.23\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.24\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.25\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.26\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.27\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.28\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.29\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.30\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.31\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.32\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.33\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.34\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.35\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.36\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.37\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.38\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.39\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.40\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.41\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.42\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.43\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.44\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.45\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.46\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.47\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.48\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.49\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.50\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.51\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.52\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.53\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.54\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.55\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.56\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.57\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.58\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.59\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.60\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.61\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.norm\\\": 1,
                \\\"lm_head\\\": 1
            }
            logger.info(f\\\"Using manual device map\\\")
            
            # Load model with optimizations
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                device_map=device_map,
                torch_dtype=torch.float32,  # Full precision
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                low_cpu_mem_usage=True
            )
            
            # Load tokenizer after model
            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                use_fast=False,
                padding_side=\\\"left\\\"
            )
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7) -> str:
        try:
            # Tokenize and move to CUDA
            inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")
            inputs = {k: v.to(self.model.device) for k, v in inputs.items()}
            
            with torch.no_grad():
                outputs = self.model.generate(
                    **inputs,
                    max_length=max_length,
                    temperature=temperature,
                    do_sample=True,
                    pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id
                )
            
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
            self.total_requests += 1
            self.total_tokens += len(outputs[0])
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            return response
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

# Initialize models dictionary
models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {}

        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",

        device_ids=[0, 1],

        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )

            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            return response
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
import os
import json
import logging
import psutil
import gc
import pynvml
from datetime import datetime
import random

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

pynvml.nvmlInit()

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class GenerateRequest(BaseModel):
    prompt: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    agent_id: str

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.device_ids = device_ids or list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.model = None
        self.tokenizer = None
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.current_device_idx = 0
    
    def load(self):
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")
            
            # Configure device mapping
            device_map = {
                \\\"model.embed_tokens\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.0\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.1\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.2\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.3\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.4\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.5\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.6\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.7\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.8\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.9\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.10\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.11\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.12\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.13\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.14\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.15\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.16\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.17\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.18\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.19\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.20\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.21\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.22\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.23\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.24\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.25\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.26\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.27\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.28\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.29\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.30\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.31\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.32\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.33\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.34\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.35\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.36\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.37\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.38\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.39\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.40\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.41\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.42\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.43\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.44\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.45\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.46\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.47\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.48\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.49\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.50\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.51\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.52\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.53\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.54\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.55\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.56\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.57\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.58\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.59\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.60\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.61\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.norm\\\": 1,
                \\\"lm_head\\\": 1
            }
            logger.info(f\\\"Using manual device map\\\")
            
            # Load model with optimizations
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                device_map=device_map,
                torch_dtype=torch.float32,  # Full precision
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                low_cpu_mem_usage=True
            )
            
            # Load tokenizer after model
            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                use_fast=False,
                padding_side=\\\"left\\\"
            )
            
            # Move tokenizer to same device as model
            self.tokenizer.pad_token = self.tokenizer.eos_token
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7) -> str:
        try:
            # Tokenize and move to CUDA
            inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")
            inputs = {k: v.to(self.model.device) for k, v in inputs.items()}
            
            with torch.no_grad():
                outputs = self.model.generate(
                    **inputs,
                    max_length=max_length,
                    temperature=temperature,
                    do_sample=True,
                    pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id
                )
            
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
            self.total_requests += 1
            self.total_tokens += len(outputs[0])
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            return response
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

# Initialize models dictionary
models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {}

# Authorized agents for initial testing
AUTHORIZED_AGENTS = set([f\\\"agent_{i}\\\" for i in range(1, 51)])  # First 50 agents

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
    logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")
    
    # Initialize multiple model instances
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1-1\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1-2\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1-3\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    # Load models
    for name, model in models.items():
        if not model.load():
            logger.error(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")
            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\", \\\"models\\\": list(models.keys())}

@app.post(\\\"/generate\\\")
async def generate(request: GenerateRequest):
    # Check agent authorization
    if request.agent_id not in AUTHORIZED_AGENTS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\\\"Agent not authorized\\\")
    
    # Simple round-robin load balancing
    model_names = list(models.keys())
    selected_model = models[random.choice(model_names)]
    
    try:
        response = selected_model.generate(
            prompt=request.prompt,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        return {\\\"response\\\": response}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

if __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
import os
import json
import logging
import psutil
import gc
import pynvml
from datetime import datetime
import random

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

pynvml.nvmlInit()

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class GenerateRequest(BaseModel):
    prompt: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    agent_id: str

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.device_ids = device_ids or list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.model = None
        self.tokenizer = None
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.current_device_idx = 0
    
    def load(self):
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")
            
            # Load model with optimizations
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                torch_dtype=torch.float16,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                low_cpu_mem_usage=True
            ).cuda()
            
            # Load tokenizer after model
            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                use_fast=False,
                padding_side=\\\"left\\\"
            )
            
            # Set pad token
            self.tokenizer.pad_token = self.tokenizer.eos_token
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7) -> str:
        try:
            # Tokenize and move to CUDA
            inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")
            inputs = {k: v.cuda() for k, v in inputs.items()}
            
            with torch.no_grad():
                outputs = self.model.generate(
                    **inputs,
                    max_length=max_length,
                    temperature=temperature,
                    do_sample=True,
                    pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id
                )
            
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
            self.total_requests += 1
            self.total_tokens += len(outputs[0])
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            return response
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

    if not models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"].load():
        logger.error(\\\"Failed to load model deepseek-r1\\\")
        raise RuntimeError(\\\"Failed to load model deepseek-r1\\\")

Let me try a different approach by using DeepSeek's own tokenizer and model classes directly:


Roo wants to execute this command:
gcloud compute ssh ethos-a3-ml --zone=us-central1-a --tunnel-through-iap --command="sudo -u ethos bash -c 'cd /llms/routing && echo \"from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
import os
import json
import logging
import psutil
import gc
import pynvml
from datetime import datetime
import random

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

pynvml.nvmlInit()

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class GenerateRequest(BaseModel):
    prompt: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    agent_id: str

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.device_ids = device_ids or list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.model = None
        self.tokenizer = None
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.current_device_idx = 0
    
    def load(self):
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")
            
            # Load tokenizer first
            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                use_fast=False,
                padding_side=\\\"left\\\"
            )
            
            # Set pad token
            self.tokenizer.pad_token = self.tokenizer.eos_token
            
            # Load model with optimizations
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                torch_dtype=torch.float16,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                low_cpu_mem_usage=True
            ).to(\\\"cuda:0\\\")
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7) -> str:
        try:
            # Tokenize
            inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")
            
            # Move inputs to same device as model
            device = next(self.model.parameters()).device
            inputs = {k: v.to(device) for k, v in inputs.items()}
            
            with torch.no_grad():
                outputs = self.model.generate(
                    **inputs,
                    max_length=max_length,
                    temperature=temperature,
                    do_sample=True,
                    pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id
                )
            
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
            self.total_requests += 1
            self.total_tokens += len(outputs[0])
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            return response
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

# Initialize models dictionary
models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {}

# Authorized agents for initial testing
AUTHORIZED_AGENTS = set([f\\\"agent_{i}\\\" for i in range(1, 51)])  # First 50 agents

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
    logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")
    
    # Initialize single model instance
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    # Load model
    if not models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"].load():
        logger.error(\\\"Failed to load model deepseek-r1\\\")
        raise RuntimeError(\\\"Failed to load model deepseek-r1\\\")

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\", \\\"models\\\": list(models.keys())}

@app.post(\\\"/generate\\\")
async def generate(request: GenerateRequest):
    # Check agent authorization
    if request.agent_id not in AUTHORIZED_AGENTS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\\\"Agent not authorized\\\")
    
    try:
        response = models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"].generate(
            prompt=request.prompt,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        return {\\\"response\\\": response}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

if __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":

Please confirm you can understand me.\\\",\\\"agent_id\\\":\\\"agent_1\\\"}\"'"

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import httpx
import json
import logging
import os
from datetime import datetime

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"R1 Extension Integration\\\")

class ExtensionRequest(BaseModel):
    extension_id: str
    prompt: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    agent_id: str

EXTENSIONS = {
    \\\"browser\\\": {
        \\\"enabled\\\": True,
        \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\",
        \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"web_search\\\", \\\"screenshot\\\", \\\"interaction\\\"]
    },
    \\\"filesystem\\\": {
        \\\"enabled\\\": True,
        \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\",
        \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"read\\\", \\\"write\\\", \\\"search\\\", \\\"list\\\"]
    },
    \\\"command\\\": {
        \\\"enabled\\\": True,
        \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\",
        \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"execute\\\", \\\"monitor\\\", \\\"control\\\"]
    },
    \\\"mcp\\\": {
        \\\"enabled\\\": True,
        \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\",
        \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"tool_use\\\", \\\"resource_access\\\"]
    }
}

PROMPT_TEMPLATES = {
    \\\"browser\\\": \\\"\\\"\\\"Browser Extension Task:
Context: {context}
Action: {action}
Parameters: {parameters}

Execute browser action and provide structured response.\\\"\\\"\\\",
    
    \\\"filesystem\\\": \\\"\\\"\\\"Filesystem Extension Task:
Context: {context}
Action: {action}
Parameters: {parameters}

Execute filesystem operation and provide structured response.\\\"\\\"\\\",
    
    \\\"command\\\": \\\"\\\"\\\"Command Extension Task:
Context: {context}
Action: {action}
Parameters: {parameters}

Execute command and provide structured response.\\\"\\\"\\\",
    
    \\\"mcp\\\": \\\"\\\"\\\"MCP Extension Task:
Context: {context}
Action: {action}
Parameters: {parameters}

Execute MCP operation and provide structured response.\\\"\\\"\\\"
}

async def forward_to_r1(prompt: str, agent_id: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
    async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
        try:
            response = await client.post(
                \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\",
                json={
                    \\\"prompt\\\": prompt,
                    \\\"agent_id\\\": agent_id,
                    \\\"max_length\\\": max_length,
                    \\\"temperature\\\": temperature
                }
            )
            response.raise_for_status()
            return response.json()
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error forwarding to R1: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.post(\\\"/extension/{extension_id}\\\")
async def handle_extension(extension_id: str, request: ExtensionRequest):
    if extension_id not in EXTENSIONS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} not found\\\")
    
    if not EXTENSIONS[extension_id][\\\"enabled\\\"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} is disabled\\\")
    
    prompt = PROMPT_TEMPLATES[extension_id].format(
        context=request.prompt,
        action=request.extension_id,
        parameters=json.dumps({
            \\\"max_length\\\": request.max_length,
            \\\"temperature\\\": request.temperature
        })
    )
    
    response = await forward_to_r1(
        prompt=prompt,
        agent_id=request.agent_id,
        max_length=request.max_length,
        temperature=request.temperature
    )
    
    return response

@app.get(\\\"/extensions\\\")
async def list_extensions():
    return {
        \\\"extensions\\\": [
            {
                \\\"id\\\": ext_id,
                \\\"enabled\\\": ext_info[\\\"enabled\\\"],
                \\\"capabilities\\\": ext_info[\\\"capabilities\\\"]
            }
            for ext_id, ext_info in EXTENSIONS.items()
        ]
    }

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\", \\\"extensions\\\": list(EXTENSIONS.keys())}

if __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":
    import uvicorn
    uvicorn.run(app, host=\\\"0.0.0.0\\\", port=8001)\" > extension_integration.py && pip install httpx && sudo systemctl start extension-integration.service && sleep 5 && sudo systemctl status extension-integration.service'"

async def forward_to_r1(prompt: str, agent_id: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
    async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Forwarding request to R1: {prompt[:100]}...\\\")
            response = await client.post(
                \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\",
                json={
                    \\\"prompt\\\": prompt,
                    \\\"agent_id\\\": agent_id,
                    \\\"max_length\\\": max_length,
                    \\\"temperature\\\": temperature
                },
                timeout=30.0
            )
            response.raise_for_status()
            return response.json()
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error forwarding to R1: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.post(\\\"/extension/{extension_id}\\\")
async def handle_extension(extension_id: str, request: ExtensionRequest):
    if extension_id not in EXTENSIONS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} not found\\\")
    
    if not EXTENSIONS[extension_id][\\\"enabled\\\"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} is disabled\\\")
    
    try:
        prompt = PROMPT_TEMPLATES[extension_id].format(
            agent_id=request.agent_id,
            action=extension_id,
            context=request.prompt
        )
        
        logger.info(f\\\"Processing {extension_id} request from {request.agent_id}\\\")
        response = await forward_to_r1(
            prompt=prompt,
            agent_id=request.agent_id,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        
        return response
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error handling extension request: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.get(\\\"/extensions\\\")
async def list_extensions():
    return {

                \\\"id\\\": ext_id,
                \\\"enabled\\\": ext_info[\\\"enabled\\\"],
                \\\"capabilities\\\": ext_info[\\\"capabilities\\\"]
            }
            for ext_id, ext_info in EXTENSIONS.items()
        ]
    }

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():

class TeamMessage(BaseModel):

            }
        }
        self.discussion_topics = {
            \\\"server_migration\\\": {
                \\\"status\\\": \\\"active\\\",
                \\\"priority\\\": \\\"high\\\",
                \\\"participants\\\": [\\\"all\\\"],
                \\\"decisions\\\": [],
                \\\"actions\\\": []
            }
        }
        self.message_history: List[TeamMessage] = []

    async def connect(self, agent_id: str, websocket: WebSocket):
        await websocket.accept()
        self.connections[agent_id] = websocket
        await self.broadcast({
            \\\"type\\\": \\\"system\\\",
            \\\"content\\\": f\\\"{agent_id} joined the coordination hub\\\",
            \\\"timestamp\\\": datetime.now().isoformat()
        })

    async def disconnect(self, agent_id: str):
        if agent_id in self.connections:
            del self.connections[agent_id]
            await self.broadcast({
                \\\"type\\\": \\\"system\\\",
                \\\"content\\\": f\\\"{agent_id} left the coordination hub\\\",
                \\\"timestamp\\\": datetime.now().isoformat()
            })

    async def broadcast(self, message: dict):
        for connection in self.connections.values():
            try:
                await connection.send_json(message)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f\\\"Error broadcasting message: {str(e)}\\\")

    def add_message_to_history(self, message: TeamMessage):
        self.message_history.append(message)
        if len(self.message_history) > 1000:
            self.message_history = self.message_history[-1000:]

team_hub = TeamHub()

@app.websocket(\\\"/ws/{agent_id}\\\")
async def websocket_endpoint(websocket: WebSocket, agent_id: str):
    await team_hub.connect(agent_id, websocket)
    try:
        while True:
            data = await websocket.receive_json()
            message = TeamMessage(**data)
            team_hub.add_message_to_history(message)
            await team_hub.broadcast(message.dict())
    except WebSocketDisconnect:
        await team_hub.disconnect(agent_id)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error in websocket connection: {str(e)}\\\")
        await team_hub.disconnect(agent_id)

@app.post(\\\"/message\\\")
async def send_message(message: TeamMessage):
    team_hub.add_message_to_history(message)
    await team_hub.broadcast(message.dict())
    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"sent\\\", \\\"timestamp\\\": message.timestamp}

@app.get(\\\"/topics\\\")
async def get_topics():
    return {\\\"topics\\\": team_hub.discussion_topics}

@app.get(\\\"/teams\\\")
async def get_teams():
    return {\\\"teams\\\": team_hub.teams}

@app.get(\\\"/history\\\")
async def get_history(limit: int = 100):
    return {\\\"history\\\": team_hub.message_history[-limit:]}

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import httpx
import json
import logging
import asyncio
from datetime import datetime

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Enhanced Nova Integration\\\")

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, name: str, endpoint: str, capabilities: List[str]):
        self.name = name
        self.endpoint = endpoint
        self.capabilities = capabilities
        self.active = True
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0

class HumanInteraction(BaseModel):
    message: str
    context: Optional[Dict] = None
    priority: str = \\\"normal\\\"
    requires_response: bool = True
    timestamp: Optional[str] = None

class ModelRequest(BaseModel):
    prompt: str
    model: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    stream: bool = False

class ExtensionRequest(BaseModel):
    extension_id: str
    prompt: str
    model: Optional[str] = None
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    agent_id: str

MODELS = {
    \\\"r1\\\": ModelConfig(
        name=\\\"R1\\\",
        endpoint=\\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\",
        capabilities=[\\\"general\\\", \\\"code\\\", \\\"analysis\\\"]
    ),
    \\\"claude\\\": ModelConfig(
        name=\\\"Claude\\\",
        endpoint=\\\"http://localhost:8001/generate\\\",
        capabilities=[\\\"general\\\", \\\"code\\\", \\\"analysis\\\", \\\"creativity\\\"]
    )
}

EXTENSIONS = {
    \\\"browser\\\": {
        \\\"enabled\\\": True,
        \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"web_search\\\", \\\"screenshot\\\", \\\"interaction\\\"]
    },
    \\\"filesystem\\\": {
        \\\"enabled\\\": True,
        \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"read\\\", \\\"write\\\", \\\"search\\\", \\\"list\\\"]
    },
    \\\"command\\\": {
        \\\"enabled\\\": True,
        \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"execute\\\", \\\"monitor\\\", \\\"control\\\"]
    },
    \\\"mcp\\\": {
        \\\"enabled\\\": True,
        \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"tool_use\\\", \\\"resource_access\\\"]
    }
}

TEAM_HUB = {
    \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8002\\\",
    \\\"ws_endpoint\\\": \\\"ws://localhost:8002/ws\\\"
}

class NovaIntegration:
    def __init__(self):
        self.models = MODELS
        self.extensions = EXTENSIONS
        self.active_connections: Dict[str, WebSocket] = {}
        self.human_interactions: List[HumanInteraction] = []
        self.model_queues: Dict[str, asyncio.Queue] = {
            model: asyncio.Queue() for model in MODELS
        }

    async def connect_to_hub(self, agent_id: str):
        async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
            try:
                async with client.websocket_connect(f\\\"{TEAM_HUB['ws_endpoint']}/{agent_id}\\\") as websocket:
                    while True:
                        data = await websocket.receive_json()
                        await self.handle_hub_message(data)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f\\\"Hub connection error: {str(e)}\\\")

    async def handle_hub_message(self, message: dict):
        if message.get(\\\"type\\\") == \\\"human_interaction\\\":
            interaction = HumanInteraction(**message)
            self.human_interactions.append(interaction)
            if interaction.requires_response:
                await self.process_human_interaction(interaction)

    async def process_human_interaction(self, interaction: HumanInteraction):
        model = self.select_model(interaction.context)
        response = await self.generate_response(
            model=model,
            prompt=self.format_interaction_prompt(interaction),
            max_length=2048
        )
        await self.send_to_hub({
            \\\"type\\\": \\\"model_response\\\",
            \\\"model\\\": model,
            \\\"interaction_id\\\": interaction.timestamp,
            \\\"response\\\": response
        })

    def select_model(self, context: Optional[Dict]) -> str:
        if not context:
            return \\\"r1\\\"
        if \\\"code\\\" in context.get(\\\"capabilities\\\", []):
            return \\\"r1\\\"
        elif \\\"creativity\\\" in context.get(\\\"capabilities\\\", []):
            return \\\"claude\\\"
        return \\\"r1\\\"

    async def generate_response(self, model: str, prompt: str, max_length: int = 2048) -> str:
        if model not in self.models:
            raise ValueError(f\\\"Unknown model: {model}\\\")
        config = self.models[model]
        async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
            response = await client.post(
                config.endpoint,
                json={
                    \\\"prompt\\\": prompt,
                    \\\"max_length\\\": max_length
                }
            )
            return response.json()

    async def send_to_hub(self, message: dict):
        async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
            await client.post(
                f\\\"{TEAM_HUB['endpoint']}/message\\\",
                json=message
            )

    def format_interaction_prompt(self, interaction: HumanInteraction) -> str:
        return f\\\"\\\"\\\"Human Interaction:
Time: {interaction.timestamp}
Priority: {interaction.priority}
Context: {json.dumps(interaction.context) if interaction.context else 'None'}

Message: {interaction.message}

Provide a helpful and relevant response.\\\"\\\"\\\"

nova = NovaIntegration()

@app.post(\\\"/generate\\\")
async def generate(request: ModelRequest):
    if request.model not in MODELS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f\\\"Model {request.model} not found\\\")
    response = await nova.generate_response(
        model=request.model,
        prompt=request.prompt,
        max_length=request.max_length
    )
    return response

@app.post(\\\"/extension/{extension_id}\\\")
async def handle_extension(extension_id: str, request: ExtensionRequest):
    if extension_id not in EXTENSIONS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} not found\\\")
    if not EXTENSIONS[extension_id][\\\"enabled\\\"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} is disabled\\\")
    model = request.model or nova.select_model({
        \\\"capabilities\\\": EXTENSIONS[extension_id][\\\"capabilities\\\"]
    })
    try:
        response = await nova.generate_response(
            model=model,
            prompt=f\\\"Extension Request ({extension_id}):\\n{request.prompt}\\\",
            max_length=request.max_length
        )
        await nova.send_to_hub({
            \\\"type\\\": \\\"extension_interaction\\\",
            \\\"extension\\\": extension_id,
            \\\"agent\\\": request.agent_id,
            \\\"model\\\": model,
            \\\"timestamp\\\": datetime.now().isoformat()
        })
        return response
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error handling extension request: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.post(\\\"/interact\\\")
async def human_interaction(interaction: HumanInteraction):
    if not interaction.timestamp:
        interaction.timestamp = datetime.now().isoformat()
    nova.human_interactions.append(interaction)
    if interaction.requires_response:
        response = await nova.process_human_interaction(interaction)
        return response
    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"received\\\", \\\"timestamp\\\": interaction.timestamp}

                \\\"capabilities\\\": model.capabilities,
                \\\"total_requests\\\": model.total_requests,

            }
            for name, model in nova.models.items()
        },
        \\\"extensions\\\": nova.extensions,
        \\\"human_interactions\\\": len(nova.human_interactions)
    }

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class GenerationRequest(BaseModel):
    prompt: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    agent_id: Optional[str] = None

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.device_ids = device_ids or list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.model = None
        self.tokenizer = None
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.current_device_idx = 0
    
    def load(self):
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")
            
            # Load tokenizer
            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True
            )
            
            # Configure device mapping for multi-GPU
            device_map = {}
            num_layers = 32  # Adjust based on model
            layers_per_device = num_layers // len(self.device_ids)
            
            for i, device_id in enumerate(self.device_ids):
                start_layer = i * layers_per_device
                end_layer = (i + 1) * layers_per_device if i < len(self.device_ids) - 1 else num_layers
                
                for layer in range(start_layer, end_layer):
                    device_map[f\\\"model.layers.{layer}\\\"] = device_id
            
            # Add special layers
            device_map[\\\"model.embed_tokens\\\"] = self.device_ids[0]
            device_map[\\\"model.norm\\\"] = self.device_ids[-1]
            device_map[\\\"lm_head\\\"] = self.device_ids[-1]
            
            logger.info(f\\\"Using device map: {device_map}\\\")
            
            # Load model with optimizations
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                device_map=device_map,
                torch_dtype=torch.float16,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                low_cpu_mem_usage=True
            )
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = None, temperature: float = 0.7) -> str:
        try:
            inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")
            inputs = {k: v.to(f\\\"cuda:{self.device_ids[0]}\\\") for k, v in inputs.items()}
            
            with torch.no_grad():
                outputs = self.model.generate(
                    **inputs,
                    max_length=max_length or self.max_length,
                    temperature=temperature,
                    pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.pad_token_id,
                    do_sample=True
                )
            
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
            self.total_requests += 1
            self.total_tokens += len(outputs[0])
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            
            return response
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

# Initialize models
models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {
    \\\"r1\\\": ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
}

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
    logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")
    
    # Load models
    for name, model in models.items():
        if not model.load():
            logger.error(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")
            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")

@app.post(\\\"/generate\\\")
async def generate(request: GenerationRequest):
    try:
        model = models[\\\"r1\\\"]  # Currently using R1 as default
        response = model.generate(
            prompt=request.prompt,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        return {\\\"response\\\": response}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error in generate endpoint: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
    return {
        \\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\",
        \\\"models\\\": {
            name: {
                \\\"loaded\\\": model.model is not None,
                \\\"last_used\\\": model.last_used,
                \\\"total_requests\\\": model.total_requests,
                \\\"total_tokens\\\": model.total_tokens
            }
            for name, model in models.items()
        }
    }

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
import os
import json
import logging
import psutil
import gc
import pynvml
from datetime import datetime

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

pynvml.nvmlInit()

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class GenerationRequest(BaseModel):
    prompt: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    agent_id: Optional[str] = None

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.device_ids = device_ids or list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.model = None
        self.tokenizer = None
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.current_device_idx = 0
    
    def load(self):
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")
            
            # Load tokenizer
            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True
            )
            
            # Configure device mapping for multi-GPU
            device_map = {}
            num_layers = 32  # Adjust based on model
            layers_per_device = num_layers // len(self.device_ids)
            
            for i, device_id in enumerate(self.device_ids):
                start_layer = i * layers_per_device
                end_layer = (i + 1) * layers_per_device if i < len(self.device_ids) - 1 else num_layers
                
                for layer in range(start_layer, end_layer):
                    device_map[f\\\"model.layers.{layer}\\\"] = device_id
            
            # Add special layers
            device_map[\\\"model.embed_tokens\\\"] = self.device_ids[0]
            device_map[\\\"model.norm\\\"] = self.device_ids[-1]
            device_map[\\\"lm_head\\\"] = self.device_ids[-1]
            
            logger.info(f\\\"Using device map: {device_map}\\\")
            
            # Load model with optimizations
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                device_map=device_map,
                torch_dtype=torch.float16,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                low_cpu_mem_usage=True
            )
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = None, temperature: float = 0.7) -> str:
        try:
            inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")
            inputs = {k: v.to(f\\\"cuda:{self.device_ids[0]}\\\") for k, v in inputs.items()}
            
            with torch.no_grad():
                outputs = self.model.generate(
                    **inputs,
                    max_length=max_length or self.max_length,
                    temperature=temperature,
                    pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.pad_token_id,
                    do_sample=True
                )
            
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
            self.total_requests += 1
            self.total_tokens += len(outputs[0])
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            
            return response
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

# Initialize models
models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
import os
import json
import logging
import psutil
import gc
import pynvml
from datetime import datetime

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

pynvml.nvmlInit()

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class GenerationRequest(BaseModel):
    prompt: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    agent_id: Optional[str] = None

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.device_ids = device_ids or [\\\"cpu\\\"]
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.model = None
        self.tokenizer = None
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.current_device_idx = 0
    
    def load(self):
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")
            
            # Load tokenizer
            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True
            )
            
            # Load model with 8-bit quantization
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                load_in_8bit=True,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True
            )
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = None, temperature: float = 0.7) -> str:
        try:
            inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")
            
            with torch.no_grad():
                outputs = self.model.generate(
                    **inputs,
                    max_length=max_length or self.max_length,
                    temperature=temperature,
                    pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.pad_token_id,
                    do_sample=True
                )
            
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
            self.total_requests += 1
            self.total_tokens += len(outputs[0])
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            
            return response
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

# Initialize models
models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {
    \\\"r1\\\": ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[\\\"cpu\\\"],
        max_length=2048
    )
}

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
    logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")
    
    # Load models
    for name, model in models.items():
        if not model.load():
            logger.error(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")
            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")

@app.post(\\\"/generate\\\")

    try:
        model = models[\\\"r1\\\"]  # Currently using R1 as default

            prompt=request.prompt,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )

    except Exception as e:

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer, BitsAndBytesConfig
import os
import json

from datetime import datetime

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class GenerationRequest(BaseModel):
    prompt: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    agent_id: Optional[str] = None

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.device_ids = device_ids or [\\\"cpu\\\"]
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.model = None
        self.tokenizer = None
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.current_device_idx = 0
    
    def load(self):
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")
            
            # Load tokenizer
            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True
            )
            
            # Configure 8-bit quantization
            quantization_config = BitsAndBytesConfig(
                load_in_8bit=True,
                llm_int8_threshold=6.0,
                llm_int8_skip_modules=None,
                llm_int8_enable_fp32_cpu_offload=True
            )
            
            # Load model with quantization
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                quantization_config=quantization_config,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True
            )
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = None, temperature: float = 0.7) -> str:
        try:
            inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")
            
            with torch.no_grad():
                outputs = self.model.generate(
                    **inputs,
                    max_length=max_length or self.max_length,
                    temperature=temperature,
                    pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.pad_token_id,
                    do_sample=True
                )
            
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
            self.total_requests += 1
            self.total_tokens += len(outputs[0])
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            
            return response
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

return True
except Exception as e:
logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
return False

def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7) -> str:
try:
# Tokenize and move to CUDA
inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")
inputs = {k: v.to(self.model.device) for k, v in inputs.items()}

with torch.no_grad():
outputs = self.model.generate(
**inputs,
max_length=max_length,
temperature=temperature,
do_sample=True,
pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id
)

response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
self.total_requests += 1
self.total_tokens += len(outputs[0])
self.last_used = datetime.now()
return response
except Exception as e:
logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
raise

# Initialize models dictionary
models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {}

# Authorized agents for initial testing
AUTHORIZED_AGENTS = set([f\\\"agent_{i}\\\" for i in range(1, 51)]) # First 50 agents

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")

# Initialize multiple model instances
models[\\\"deepseek-r1-1\\\"] = ModelConfig(
model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
device_ids=[0, 1],
max_length=2048
)

)

models[\\\"deepseek-r1-3\\\"] = ModelConfig(
model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
device_ids=[0, 1],
max_length=2048
)

# Load models
for name, model in models.items():
if not model.load():
logger.error(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")
raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\", \\\"models\\\": list(models.keys())}

@app.post(\\\"/generate\\\")
async def generate(request: GenerateRequest):
# Check agent authorization
if request.agent_id not in AUTHORIZED_AGENTS:
raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\\\"Agent not authorized\\\")

prompt=request.prompt,
max_length=request.max_length,
temperature=request.temperature
)
return {\\\"response\\\": response}
except Exception as e:
logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

class GenerateRequest(BaseModel):
