logger.info(f\\\"Using manual device map\\\")

# Load model with optimizations
self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
self.model_path,
device_map=device_map,
torch_dtype=torch.float32, # Full precision
trust_remote_code=True,
local_files_only=True,
low_cpu_mem_usage=True
)

# Load tokenizer after model
self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
self.model_path,
trust_remote_code=True,
local_files_only=True,
use_fast=False,
padding_side=\\\"left\\\"
)

# Move tokenizer to same device as model
self.tokenizer.pad_token = self.tokenizer.eos_token

self.last_used = datetime.now()
logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
return True
except Exception as e:
logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
return False

def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7) -> str:
try:
# Tokenize and move to CUDA
inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")
inputs = {k: v.to(self.model.device) for k, v in inputs.items()}

with torch.no_grad():
outputs = self.model.generate(
**inputs,
max_length=max_length,
temperature=temperature,
do_sample=True,
pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id
)

response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
self.total_requests += 1
self.total_tokens += len(outputs[0])
self.last_used = datetime.now()
return response
except Exception as e:
logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
raise

models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {}

# Authorized agents for initial testing
AUTHORIZED_AGENTS = set([f\\\"agent_{i}\\\" for i in range(1, 51)]) # First 50 agents

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")

logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")

# Initialize single model instance
models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"] = ModelConfig(
model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
device_ids=[0, 1],
max_length=2048
)

# Load model
if not models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"].load():
logger.error(\\\"Failed to load model deepseek-r1\\\")
raise RuntimeError(\\\"Failed to load model deepseek-r1\\\")

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\", \\\"models\\\": list(models.keys())}

@app.post(\\\"/generate\\\")
async def generate(request: GenerateRequest):
# Check agent authorization
if request.agent_id not in AUTHORIZED_AGENTS:
raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\\\"Agent not authorized\\\")

try:
response = models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"].generate(
prompt=request.prompt,
max_length=request.max_length,
temperature=request.temperature
)
return {\\\"response\\\": response}
except Exception as e:
logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
import os
import json
import logging
import psutil
import gc
import pynvml
from datetime import datetime
import random

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

pynvml.nvmlInit()

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class GenerateRequest(BaseModel):
prompt: str
max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
agent_id: str

class ModelConfig:
def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
self.model_id = model_id
self.model_path = model_path
self.device_ids = device_ids or list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))
self.max_length = max_length
self.model = None
self.tokenizer = None
self.last_used = None
self.total_requests = 0
self.total_tokens = 0
self.current_device_idx = 0

def load(self):
try:
logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")

# Load tokenizer first
self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
self.model_path,
trust_remote_code=True,
local_files_only=True,
use_fast=False,
padding_side=\\\"left\\\"
)

# Set pad token
self.tokenizer.pad_token = self.tokenizer.eos_token

# Load model with optimizations
self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
self.model_path,
torch_dtype=torch.float16,
trust_remote_code=True,
local_files_only=True,
low_cpu_mem_usage=True
).to(\\\"cuda:0\\\")

self.last_used = datetime.now()
logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
return True
except Exception as e:
logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
return False

def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7) -> str:
try:
# Tokenize
inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")

# Move inputs to same device as model
device = next(self.model.parameters()).device
inputs = {k: v.to(device) for k, v in inputs.items()}

with torch.no_grad():
outputs = self.model.generate(
**inputs,
max_length=max_length,
temperature=temperature,
do_sample=True,
pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id
)

response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
self.total_requests += 1
self.total_tokens += len(outputs[0])
self.last_used = datetime.now()
return response
except Exception as e:
logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
raise

how do we connect to it?



Let me show you how to connect to the model router service:


Roo wants to execute this command:
gcloud compute ssh ethos-a3-ml --zone=us-central1-a --tunnel-through-iap -- -L 8000:localhost:8000 -N -f


Task Completed
The model router service is now accessible through port forwarding. You can connect to it using:

Health check:
curl http://localhost:8000/health
Generate text:
curl -X POST http://localhost:8000/generate \
-H "Content-Type: application/json" \
-d '{"prompt": "Your prompt here", "agent_id": "agent_1"}'
The service is running on localhost:8000 and accepts POST requests to /generate with a JSON body containing:

prompt: The text prompt to generate from
agent_id: Must be in format "agent_N" where N is 1-50
max_length (optional): Maximum length of generated text (default 2048)
temperature (optional): Controls randomness (default 0.7)

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
import os
import json
import logging
import psutil
import gc
import pynvml
from datetime import datetime
import random

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

pynvml.nvmlInit()

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class GenerateRequest(BaseModel):
prompt: str
max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
agent_id: str

class ModelConfig:
def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
self.model_id = model_id
self.model_path = model_path
self.device_ids = device_ids or list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))
self.max_length = max_length
self.model = None
self.tokenizer = None
self.last_used = None
self.total_requests = 0
self.total_tokens = 0
self.current_device_idx = 0

def load(self):
try:
logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")

# Load tokenizer first
self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
self.model_path,
trust_remote_code=True,
local_files_only=True,
use_fast=False,
padding_side=\\\"left\\\"
)

# Set pad token
self.tokenizer.pad_token = self.tokenizer.eos_token

# Load model with optimizations
self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
self.model_path,
torch_dtype=torch.float16,
trust_remote_code=True,
local_files_only=True,
low_cpu_mem_usage=True
).to(\\\"cuda:0\\\")

self.last_used = datetime.now()
logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
return True
except Exception as e:
logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
return False

def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7) -> str:
try:
# Tokenize
inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")

# Move inputs to same device as model
device = next(self.model.parameters()).device
inputs = {k: v.to(device) for k, v in inputs.items()}

with torch.no_grad():
outputs = self.model.generate(
**inputs,
max_length=max_length,
temperature=temperature,
do_sample=True,
pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id
)

response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
self.total_requests += 1
self.total_tokens += len(outputs[0])
self.last_used = datetime.now()
return response
except Exception as e:
logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
raise

# Initialize models dictionary
models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {}

# Authorized agents
AUTHORIZED_AGENTS = {
# Standard agents
*[f\\\"agent_{i}\\\" for i in range(1, 51)],
# Nova agents
*[f\\\"nova_{i}\\\" for i in range(1, 11)],
# Team agents
\\\"commsops_pathfinder\\\",
\\\"memops_echo\\\",
\\\"memops_nexus\\\",
\\\"ethos_zenith\\\"
}

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")

# Initialize single model instance
models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"] = ModelConfig(
model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
device_ids=[0],
max_length=2048
)

# Load model
if not models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"].load():
logger.error(\\\"Failed to load model deepseek-r1\\\")
raise RuntimeError(\\\"Failed to load model deepseek-r1\\\")

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\", \\\"models\\\": list(models.keys())}

@app.post(\\\"/generate\\\")
async def generate(request: GenerateRequest):
# Check agent authorization
if request.agent_id not in AUTHORIZED_AGENTS:
raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\\\"Agent not authorized\\\")

try:
response = models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"].generate(
prompt=request.prompt,
max_length=request.max_length,
temperature=request.temperature
)
return {\\\"response\\\": response}
except Exception as e:
logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

if __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":
import uvicorn
uvicorn.run(app, host=\\\"0.0.0.0\\\", port=8000)\" > model_router.py && sudo systemctl restart model-router.service'"

# DeepSeek-R1 Download Migration Commands

## 1. Check and Pause Download

```bash
# Check running processes
ps aux | grep -i "huggingface\|git lfs"

# Kill download processes
sudo pkill -f "huggingface-cli"
sudo pkill -f "git lfs"

# Verify processes are stopped
ps aux | grep -i "huggingface\|git lfs"

# Sync filesystem to ensure all writes are complete
sync
```

## 2. Verify Current State

```bash
# Check current size and integrity
du -sh /novas/models/DeepSeek-R1
find /novas/models/DeepSeek-R1 -type f -name "*.incomplete" -ls
md5sum /novas/models/DeepSeek-R1/* > /novas/models/pre_move_checksums.md5
```

## 3. Prepare for Move

```bash
# Create target directory on new system
ssh ethos "sudo mkdir -p /novas/models && sudo chown -R $USER:$USER /novas/models"

# Verify target disk space
ssh ethos "df -h /novas"
```

## 4. Transfer Files

```bash
# Using rsync with resume capability and progress monitoring
rsync -avzP --partial \
--bwlimit=186M \ # Match new system's optimal speed
/novas/models/DeepSeek-R1/ \
ethos:/novas/models/DeepSeek-R1/

# Alternative: Using netcat for raw speed (if systems are directly connected)
tar czf - /novas/models/DeepSeek-R1 | pv -s 623G | \
nc -w 3 ethos 9999
```

## 5. Verify Transfer

```bash
# Check size on new system
ssh ethos "du -sh /novas/models/DeepSeek-R1"

# Verify checksums
ssh ethos "cd /novas/models && md5sum -c pre_move_checksums.md5"
```

## 6. Resume Download

```bash
# On new system (ethos)
cd /novas/models/DeepSeek-R1
source /llms/venv/bin/activate
huggingface-cli download deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-R1 --resume-download --local-dir .
```

## 7. Cleanup

```bash
# After successful transfer and resume
rm /novas/models/pre_move_checksums.md5
```

## Recovery Commands (if needed)

```bash
# If rsync fails
rsync --partial-dir=.rsync-partial --append-verify -avzP \
/novas/models/DeepSeek-R1/ \
ethos:/novas/models/DeepSeek-R1/

# If transfer corrupted
rsync --delete -avzP \
/novas/models/DeepSeek-R1/ \
ethos:/novas/models/DeepSeek-R1/
```

## Notes
- Current size: 623GB/720GB
- Target system speed: 186MB/s
- Remaining download: ~97GB
- Estimated completion time after move: ~8.7 minutes at full speedx@compute:/data/ax/MLOps/aiml$

Let me install accelerate and update the model router code to handle GPU loading properly:

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
import os
import json
import logging
import psutil
import gc
import pynvml
from datetime import datetime

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

pynvml.nvmlInit()

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class GenerateRequest(BaseModel):
    prompt: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    agent_id: str

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.device_ids = device_ids or list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.model = None
        self.tokenizer = None
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.current_device_idx = 0
    
    def load(self):
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")
            
            # Load tokenizer
            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True
            )
            
            # Load model with GPU support
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                torch_dtype=torch.float16,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                device_map=\\\"auto\\\"
            )
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = None, temperature: float = 0.7) -> str:
        try:
            # Tokenize
            inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")
            
            # Move inputs to same device as model
            device = next(self.model.parameters()).device
            inputs = {k: v.to(device) for k, v in inputs.items()}
            
            with torch.no_grad():
                outputs = self.model.generate(
                    **inputs,
                    max_length=max_length or self.max_length,
                    temperature=temperature,
                    do_sample=True,
                    pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id
                )
            
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
            self.total_requests += 1
            self.total_tokens += len(outputs[0])
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            return response
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

# Initialize models dictionary
models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {}

# Authorized agents
AUTHORIZED_AGENTS = {
    # Standard agents
    *[f\\\"agent_{i}\\\" for i in range(1, 51)],
    # Nova agents
    *[f\\\"nova_{i}\\\" for i in range(1, 11)],
    # Team agents
    \\\"commsops_pathfinder\\\",
    \\\"memops_echo\\\",
    \\\"memops_nexus\\\",
    \\\"ethos_zenith\\\"
}

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
    logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")
    
    # Initialize single model instance
    models[\\\"r1\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/novas/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/novas/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    # Load model
    if not models[\\\"r1\\\"].load():
        logger.error(\\\"Failed to load model r1\\\")
        raise RuntimeError(\\\"Failed to load model r1\\\")

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
    return {
        \\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\",
        \\\"models\\\": {
            name: {
                \\\"loaded\\\": model.model is not None,
                \\\"last_used\\\": model.last_used,
                \\\"total_requests\\\": model.total_requests,
                \\\"total_tokens\\\": model.total_tokens
            }
            for name, model in models.items()
        }
    }

@app.post(\\\"/generate\\\")
async def generate(request: GenerateRequest):
    # Check agent authorization
    if request.agent_id not in AUTHORIZED_AGENTS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\\\"Agent not authorized\\\")
    
    try:
        response = models[\\\"r1\\\"].generate(
            prompt=request.prompt,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        return {\\\"response\\\": response}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

if __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":
    import uvicorn
    uvicorn.run(app, host=\\\"0.0.0.0\\\", port=8000)\" > model_router.py && sudo systemctl restart model-router.service && sleep 5 && sudo journalctl -u model-router.service -n 50 --no-pager'"
Command Output

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import httpx
import json
import logging
import os
from datetime import datetime

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

app = FastAPI(title=\"R1 Extension Integration\")

class ExtensionRequest(BaseModel):
    extension_id: str
    prompt: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    agent_id: str

EXTENSIONS = {
    \"browser\": {
        \"enabled\": True,
        \"endpoint\": \"http://localhost:8000/generate\",
        \"capabilities\": [\"web_search\", \"screenshot\", \"interaction\"]
    },
    \"filesystem\": {
        \"enabled\": True,
        \"endpoint\": \"http://localhost:8000/generate\",
        \"capabilities\": [\"read\", \"write\", \"search\", \"list\"]
    },
    \"command\": {
        \"enabled\": True,
        \"endpoint\": \"http://localhost:8000/generate\",
        \"capabilities\": [\"execute\", \"monitor\", \"control\"]
    },
    \"mcp\": {
        \"enabled\": True,
        \"endpoint\": \"http://localhost:8000/generate\",
        \"capabilities\": [\"tool_use\", \"resource_access\"]
    }
}

PROMPT_TEMPLATES = {
    \"browser\": \"\"\"Task: Browser Extension Operation
Agent: {agent_id}
Action: {action}
Context: {context}

You are an AI agent with browser capabilities. Execute the requested browser operation and provide a structured response.\"\"\",
    
    \"filesystem\": \"\"\"Task: Filesystem Operation
Agent: {agent_id}
Action: {action}
Context: {context}

You are an AI agent with filesystem capabilities. Execute the requested filesystem operation and provide a structured response.\"\"\",
    
    \"command\": \"\"\"Task: Command Execution
Agent: {agent_id}
Action: {action}
Context: {context}

You are an AI agent with command execution capabilities. Execute the requested command and provide a structured response.\"\"\",
    
    \"mcp\": \"\"\"Task: MCP Operation
Agent: {agent_id}
Action: {action}
Context: {context}

You are an AI agent with MCP capabilities. Execute the requested MCP operation and provide a structured response.\"\"\"
}

async def forward_to_r1(prompt: str, agent_id: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
    """Forward request to R1 model"""
    async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
        try:
            logger.info(f\"Forwarding request to R1: {prompt[:100]}...\")
            response = await client.post(
                \"http://localhost:8000/generate\",
                json={
                    \"prompt\": prompt,
                    \"agent_id\": agent_id,
                    \"max_length\": max_length,
                    \"temperature\": temperature
                },
                timeout=30.0
            )
            response.raise_for_status()
            return response.json()
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\"Error forwarding to R1: {str(e)}\")
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.post(\"/extension/{extension_id}\")
async def handle_extension(extension_id: str, request: ExtensionRequest):
    """Handle extension requests"""
    if extension_id not in EXTENSIONS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f\"Extension {extension_id} not found\")
    
    if not EXTENSIONS[extension_id][\"enabled\"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=f\"Extension {extension_id} is disabled\")
    
    try:
        # Format prompt using template
        prompt = PROMPT_TEMPLATES[extension_id].format(
            agent_id=request.agent_id,
            action=extension_id,
            context=request.prompt
        )
        
        # Forward to R1
        logger.info(f\"Processing {extension_id} request from {request.agent_id}\")
        response = await forward_to_r1(
            prompt=prompt,
            agent_id=request.agent_id,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        
        return response
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\"Error handling extension request: {str(e)}\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.get(\"/extensions\")
async def list_extensions():
    """List available extensions"""
    return {
        \"extensions\": [
            {
                \"id\": ext_id,
                \"enabled\": ext_info[\"enabled\"],
                \"capabilities\": ext_info[\"capabilities\"]
            }
            for ext_id, ext_info in EXTENSIONS.items()
        ]
    }

@app.get(\"/health\")
async def health_check():
    """Health check endpoint"""
    return {\"status\": \"healthy\", \"extensions\": list(EXTENSIONS.keys())}

if __name__ == \"__main__\":
    import uvicorn
    logger.info(\"Starting R1 Extension Integration Service\")
    uvicorn.run(app, host=\"0.0.0.0\", port=8001)
EOL
cd /llms/routing && source /novas/torch_venv/bin/activate && python3 extensions/extension_integration.py"

@app.post(\"/extension/{extension_id}\")
async def handle_extension(extension_id: str, request: ExtensionRequest):
    """Handle extension requests"""
    if extension_id not in EXTENSIONS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f\"Extension {extension_id} not found\")
    
    if not EXTENSIONS[extension_id][\"enabled\"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=f\"Extension {extension_id} is disabled\")
    
    try:

            agent_id=request.agent_id,
            action=extension_id,
            context=request.prompt

        response = await forward_to_r1(
            prompt=prompt,
            agent_id=request.agent_id,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        
        return response
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\"Error handling extension request: {str(e)}\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.post('/extension/{extension_id}')
async def handle_extension(extension_id: str, request: ExtensionRequest):
    """Handle extension requests"""
    if extension_id not in EXTENSIONS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f'Extension {extension_id} not found')
    
    if not EXTENSIONS[extension_id]['enabled']:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=f'Extension {extension_id} is disabled')
    
    try:
        # Format prompt using template
        prompt = PROMPT_TEMPLATES[extension_id].format(
            agent_id=request.agent_id,
            action=extension_id,
            context=request.prompt
        )
        
        # Forward to R1
        logger.info(f'Processing {extension_id} request from {request.agent_id}')
        response = await forward_to_r1(
            prompt=prompt,
            agent_id=request.agent_id,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        
        return response
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f'Error handling extension request: {str(e)}')
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.get('/extensions')
async def list_extensions():
    """List available extensions"""
    return {
        'extensions': [
            {
                'id': ext_id,
                'enabled': ext_info['enabled'],
                'capabilities': ext_info['capabilities']
            }
            for ext_id, ext_info in EXTENSIONS.items()
        ]
    }

@app.get('/health')
async def health_check():
    """Health check endpoint"""
    return {'status': 'healthy', 'extensions': list(EXTENSIONS.keys())}

    logger.info('Starting R1 Extension Integration Service')

async def forward_to_r1(prompt: str, agent_id: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
    """Forward request to R1 model"""
    async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
        try:
            logger.info(f"Forwarding request to R1: {prompt[:100]}...")
            response = await client.post(
                "http://localhost:8000/generate",
                json={
                    "prompt": prompt,
                    "agent_id": agent_id,
                    "max_length": max_length,
                    "temperature": temperature
                },
                timeout=30.0
            )
            response.raise_for_status()
            return response.json()
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error forwarding to R1: {str(e)}")
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import httpx
import json
import logging
import os
from datetime import datetime

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

app = FastAPI(title='R1 Extension Integration')

class ExtensionRequest(BaseModel):
    extension_id: str
    prompt: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    agent_id: str

EXTENSIONS = {
    'browser': {
        'enabled': True,
        'endpoint': 'http://localhost:8000/generate',
        'capabilities': ['web_search', 'screenshot', 'interaction']
    },
    'filesystem': {
        'enabled': True,
        'endpoint': 'http://localhost:8000/generate',
        'capabilities': ['read', 'write', 'search', 'list']
    },
    'command': {
        'enabled': True,
        'endpoint': 'http://localhost:8000/generate',
        'capabilities': ['execute', 'monitor', 'control']
    },
    'mcp': {
        'enabled': True,
        'endpoint': 'http://localhost:8000/generate',
        'capabilities': ['tool_use', 'resource_access']
    }
}

PROMPT_TEMPLATES = {
    'browser': '''Task: Browser Extension Operation
Agent: {agent_id}
Action: {action}
Context: {context}

You are an AI agent with browser capabilities. Execute the requested browser operation and provide a structured response.''',
    
    'filesystem': '''Task: Filesystem Operation
Agent: {agent_id}
Action: {action}
Context: {context}

You are an AI agent with filesystem capabilities. Execute the requested filesystem operation and provide a structured response.''',
    
    'command': '''Task: Command Execution
Agent: {agent_id}
Action: {action}
Context: {context}

You are an AI agent with command execution capabilities. Execute the requested command and provide a structured response.''',
    
    'mcp': '''Task: MCP Operation
Agent: {agent_id}
Action: {action}
Context: {context}

You are an AI agent with MCP capabilities. Execute the requested MCP operation and provide a structured response.'''
}

async def forward_to_r1(prompt: str, agent_id: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
    """Forward request to R1 model"""
    async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
        try:
            logger.info(f'Forwarding request to R1: {prompt[:100]}...')
            response = await client.post(
                'http://localhost:8000/generate',
                json={
                    'prompt': prompt,
                    'agent_id': agent_id,
                    'max_length': max_length,
                    'temperature': temperature
                },
                timeout=30.0
            )
            response.raise_for_status()
            data = response.json()
            logger.info(f'Got response from R1: {data}')
            return data
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f'Error forwarding to R1: {str(e)}')
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.post('/extension/{extension_id}')
async def handle_extension(extension_id: str, request: ExtensionRequest):
    """Handle extension requests"""
    if extension_id not in EXTENSIONS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f'Extension {extension_id} not found')
    
    if not EXTENSIONS[extension_id]['enabled']:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=f'Extension {extension_id} is disabled')
    
    try:
        # Format prompt using template
        prompt = PROMPT_TEMPLATES[extension_id].format(
            agent_id=request.agent_id,
            action=extension_id,
            context=request.prompt
        )
        
        # Forward to R1
        logger.info(f'Processing {extension_id} request from {request.agent_id}')
        response = await forward_to_r1(
            prompt=prompt,
            agent_id=request.agent_id,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        
        return response
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f'Error handling extension request: {str(e)}')
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.get('/extensions')
async def list_extensions():
    """List available extensions"""
    return {
        'extensions': [
            {
                'id': ext_id,
                'enabled': ext_info['enabled'],
                'capabilities': ext_info['capabilities']
            }
            for ext_id, ext_info in EXTENSIONS.items()
        ]
    }

@app.get('/health')
async def health_check():
    """Health check endpoint"""
    return {'status': 'healthy', 'extensions': list(EXTENSIONS.keys())}

if __name__ == '__main__':
    import uvicorn
    logger.info('Starting R1 Extension Integration Service')
    uvicorn.run(app, host='0.0.0.0', port=8001)

        try:

@app.post(\\\"/extension/{extension_id}\\\")
async def handle_extension(extension_id: str, request: ExtensionRequest):
    if extension_id not in EXTENSIONS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} not found\\\")
    if not EXTENSIONS[extension_id][\\\"enabled\\\"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} is disabled\\\")
    try:
        prompt = PROMPT_TEMPLATES[extension_id].format(agent_id=request.agent_id, action=extension_id, context=request.prompt)
        logger.info(f\\\"Processing {extension_id} request from {request.agent_id}\\\")
        response = await forward_to_r1(prompt=prompt, agent_id=request.agent_id, max_length=request.max_length, temperature=request.temperature)
        return response
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error handling extension request: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import httpx
import json
import logging
import os
from datetime import datetime

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

app = FastAPI(title='R1 Extension Integration')

class ExtensionRequest(BaseModel):
    extension_id: str
    prompt: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    agent_id: str

EXTENSIONS = {
    'browser': {'enabled': True, 'endpoint': 'http://localhost:8000/generate', 'capabilities': ['web_search', 'screenshot', 'interaction']},
    'filesystem': {'enabled': True, 'endpoint': 'http://localhost:8000/generate', 'capabilities': ['read', 'write', 'search', 'list']},
    'command': {'enabled': True, 'endpoint': 'http://localhost:8000/generate', 'capabilities': ['execute', 'monitor', 'control']},
    'mcp': {'enabled': True, 'endpoint': 'http://localhost:8000/generate', 'capabilities': ['tool_use', 'resource_access']}
}

PROMPT_TEMPLATES = {
    'browser': 'Task: Browser Extension Operation\nAgent: {agent_id}\nAction: {action}\nContext: {context}\n\nYou are an AI agent with browser capabilities. Execute the requested browser operation and provide a structured response.',
    'filesystem': 'Task: Filesystem Operation\nAgent: {agent_id}\nAction: {action}\nContext: {context}\n\nYou are an AI agent with filesystem capabilities. Execute the requested filesystem operation and provide a structured response.',
    'command': 'Task: Command Execution\nAgent: {agent_id}\nAction: {action}\nContext: {context}\n\nYou are an AI agent with command execution capabilities. Execute the requested command and provide a structured response.',
    'mcp': 'Task: MCP Operation\nAgent: {agent_id}\nAction: {action}\nContext: {context}\n\nYou are an AI agent with MCP capabilities. Execute the requested MCP operation and provide a structured response.'
}

async def forward_to_r1(prompt: str, agent_id: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
    async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
        try:
            logger.info(f'Forwarding request to R1: {prompt[:100]}...')
            response = await client.post(
                'http://localhost:8000/generate',
                json={
                    'prompt': prompt,
                    'agent_id': agent_id,
                    'max_length': max_length,
                    'temperature': temperature
                },
                timeout=30.0
            )
            response.raise_for_status()
            data = response.json()
            logger.info(f'Got response from R1: {data}')
            return data
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f'Error forwarding to R1: {str(e)}')
            if isinstance(e, httpx.HTTPError):
                logger.error(f'Response content: {e.response.content if hasattr(e, "response") else "No response"}')
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.post('/extension/{extension_id}')
async def handle_extension(extension_id: str, request: ExtensionRequest):
    if extension_id not in EXTENSIONS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f'Extension {extension_id} not found')
    if not EXTENSIONS[extension_id]['enabled']:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=f'Extension {extension_id} is disabled')
    try:
        prompt = PROMPT_TEMPLATES[extension_id].format(
            agent_id=request.agent_id,
            action=extension_id,
            context=request.prompt
        )
        logger.info(f'Processing {extension_id} request from {request.agent_id}')
        response = await forward_to_r1(
            prompt=prompt,
            agent_id=request.agent_id,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        return response
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f'Error handling extension request: {str(e)}')
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.get('/extensions')
async def list_extensions():
    return {'extensions': [{'id': ext_id, 'enabled': ext_info['enabled'], 'capabilities': ext_info['capabilities']} for ext_id, ext_info in EXTENSIONS.items()]}

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):

        self.model_path = model_path

        self.last_used = None

        self.total_tokens = 0

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
    logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")
    
    # Initialize model
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    # Load models
    for name, model in models.items():
        if not model.load():
            logger.error(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")
            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
import os
import json
import logging
import psutil
import gc
import pynvml
from datetime import datetime

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

pynvml.nvmlInit()

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class GenerateRequest(BaseModel):
    prompt: str
    agent_id: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.device_ids = device_ids or list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.model = None
        self.tokenizer = None
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.current_device_idx = 0
    
    def load(self):
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")
            
            # Load tokenizer with local caching
            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True
            )
            
            # Configure device mapping
            device_map = {
                \\\"model.embed_tokens\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.0\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.1\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.2\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.3\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.4\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.5\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.6\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.7\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.8\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.9\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.10\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.11\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.12\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.13\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.14\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.15\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.16\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.17\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.18\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.19\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.20\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.21\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.22\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.23\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.24\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.25\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.26\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.27\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.28\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.29\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.30\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.31\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.32\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.33\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.34\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.35\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.36\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.37\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.38\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.39\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.40\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.41\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.42\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.43\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.44\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.45\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.46\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.47\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.48\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.49\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.50\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.51\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.52\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.53\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.54\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.55\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.56\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.57\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.58\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.59\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.60\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.61\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.norm\\\": 1,
                \\\"lm_head\\\": 1
            }
            logger.info(f\\\"Using manual device map\\\")
            
            # Load model with optimizations
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                device_map=device_map,
                torch_dtype=torch.float16,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                low_cpu_mem_usage=True
            )
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
        try:
            inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\").to(\\\"cuda\\\")
            outputs = self.model.generate(
                **inputs,
                max_length=max_length,
                temperature=temperature,
                do_sample=True,
                pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id
            )
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
            return response
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

# Initialize models dictionary
models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {}

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
    logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")
    
    # Initialize model
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    # Load models
    for name, model in models.items():
        if not model.load():
            logger.error(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")
            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")

@app.post(\\\"/generate\\\")
async def generate(request: GenerateRequest):
    try:
        model = models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"]
        response = model.generate(
            prompt=request.prompt,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        return {\\\"response\\\": response}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error handling request: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\", \\\"models\\\": list(models.keys())}

if __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":
    import uvicorn
    uvicorn.run(app, host=\\\"0.0.0.0\\\", port=8000)\" > model_router.py && sudo systemctl restart model-router.service && sleep 5 && sudo journalctl -u model-router.service -n 50 --no-pager'"

from pydantic import BaseModel

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class GenerateRequest(BaseModel):
    prompt: str
    agent_id: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.device_ids = device_ids or list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.model = None
        self.tokenizer = None
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.current_device_idx = 0
    
    def load(self):
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")

            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True
            )

            device_map = {

                \\\"model.layers.0\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.1\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.2\\\": 0,

                \\\"model.layers.21\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.22\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.23\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.24\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.25\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.26\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.27\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.28\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.29\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.30\\\": 0,
                \\\"model.layers.31\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.32\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.33\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.34\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.35\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.36\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.37\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.38\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.39\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.40\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.41\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.42\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.43\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.44\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.45\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.46\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.47\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.48\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.49\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.50\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.51\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.52\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.53\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.54\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.55\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.56\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.57\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.58\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.59\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.60\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.layers.61\\\": 1,
                \\\"model.norm\\\": 1,
                \\\"lm_head\\\": 1
            }
            logger.info(f\\\"Using manual device map\\\")
            
            # Load model with optimizations
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                device_map=device_map,
                torch_dtype=torch.float16,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                low_cpu_mem_usage=True
            )
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
        try:
            inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")
            # Move input tensors to the same device as the model
            inputs = {k: v.to(self.model.device) for k, v in inputs.items()}
            outputs = self.model.generate(
                **inputs,
                max_length=max_length,
                temperature=temperature,
                do_sample=True,
                pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id
            )
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
            return response
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

# Initialize models dictionary
models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {}

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
    logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")
    
    # Initialize model
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    # Load models
    for name, model in models.items():
        if not model.load():
            logger.error(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")
            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")

@app.post(\\\"/generate\\\")
async def generate(request: GenerateRequest):
    try:
        model = models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"]
        response = model.generate(
            prompt=request.prompt,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        return {\\\"response\\\": response}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error handling request: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\", \\\"models\\\": list(models.keys())}

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
import os
import json
import logging
import psutil
import gc
import pynvml
from datetime import datetime

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

pynvml.nvmlInit()

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class GenerateRequest(BaseModel):
    prompt: str
    agent_id: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.device_ids = device_ids or list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.model = None
        self.tokenizer = None
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.current_device_idx = 0
    
    def load(self):
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")
            
            # Load tokenizer with local caching
            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True
            )
            
            # Configure device mapping
            device_map = \\\"auto\\\"
            logger.info(f\\\"Using automatic device map\\\")
            
            # Load model with optimizations
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                device_map=device_map,
                torch_dtype=torch.float16,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                low_cpu_mem_usage=True
            )
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
        try:
            # Get the device of the first parameter of the model
            device = next(self.model.parameters()).device
            logger.info(f\\\"Using device: {device}\\\")
            
            inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")
            # Move input tensors to the same device as the model
            inputs = {k: v.to(device) for k, v in inputs.items()}
            
            outputs = self.model.generate(
                **inputs,
                max_length=max_length,
                temperature=temperature,
                do_sample=True,
                pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id
            )
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
            return response
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

# Initialize models dictionary
models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {}

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
    logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")
    
    # Initialize model
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    # Load models
    for name, model in models.items():
        if not model.load():
            logger.error(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")
            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import httpx
import json
import logging
import os
from datetime import datetime

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"R1 Extension Integration\\\")

class ExtensionRequest(BaseModel):
    extension_id: str
    prompt: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    agent_id: str

EXTENSIONS = {
    \\\"browser\\\": {\\\"enabled\\\": True, \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\", \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"web_search\\\", \\\"screenshot\\\", \\\"interaction\\\"]},
    \\\"filesystem\\\": {\\\"enabled\\\": True, \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\", \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"read\\\", \\\"write\\\", \\\"search\\\", \\\"list\\\"]},
    \\\"command\\\": {\\\"enabled\\\": True, \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\", \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"execute\\\", \\\"monitor\\\", \\\"control\\\"]},
    \\\"mcp\\\": {\\\"enabled\\\": True, \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\", \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"tool_use\\\", \\\"resource_access\\\"]}
}

PROMPT_TEMPLATES = {
    \\\"browser\\\": \\\"Task: Browser Extension Operation\\\\nAgent: {agent_id}\\\\nAction: {action}\\\\nContext: {context}\\\\n\\\\nYou are an AI agent with browser capabilities. Execute the requested browser operation and provide a structured response.\\\",
    \\\"filesystem\\\": \\\"Task: Filesystem Operation\\\\nAgent: {agent_id}\\\\nAction: {action}\\\\nContext: {context}\\\\n\\\\nYou are an AI agent with filesystem capabilities. Execute the requested filesystem operation and provide a structured response.\\\",
    \\\"command\\\": \\\"Task: Command Execution\\\\nAgent: {agent_id}\\\\nAction: {action}\\\\nContext: {context}\\\\n\\\\nYou are an AI agent with command execution capabilities. Execute the requested command and provide a structured response.\\\",
    \\\"mcp\\\": \\\"Task: MCP Operation\\\\nAgent: {agent_id}\\\\nAction: {action}\\\\nContext: {context}\\\\n\\\\nYou are an AI agent with MCP capabilities. Execute the requested MCP operation and provide a structured response.\\\"
}

async def forward_to_r1(prompt: str, agent_id: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
    async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Forwarding request to R1: {prompt[:100]}...\\\")
            response = await client.post(
                \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\",
                json={
                    \\\"prompt\\\": prompt,
                    \\\"agent_id\\\": agent_id,
                    \\\"max_length\\\": max_length,
                    \\\"temperature\\\": temperature
                },
                timeout=30.0
            )
            response.raise_for_status()
            data = response.json()
            logger.info(f\\\"Got response from R1: {data}\\\")
            return data
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error forwarding to R1: {str(e)}\\\")
            if isinstance(e, httpx.HTTPError):
                logger.error(f\\\"Response content: {e.response.content if hasattr(e, \\\"response\\\") else \\\"No response\\\"}\\\")
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.post(\\\"/extension/{extension_id}\\\")
async def handle_extension(extension_id: str, request: ExtensionRequest):
    if extension_id not in EXTENSIONS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} not found\\\")
    if not EXTENSIONS[extension_id][\\\"enabled\\\"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} is disabled\\\")
    try:
        prompt = PROMPT_TEMPLATES[extension_id].format(
            agent_id=request.agent_id,
            action=extension_id,
            context=request.prompt
        )
        logger.info(f\\\"Processing {extension_id} request from {request.agent_id}\\\")
        response = await forward_to_r1(
            prompt=prompt,
            agent_id=request.agent_id,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        return response
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error handling extension request: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.get(\\\"/extensions\\\")
async def list_extensions():
    return {\\\"extensions\\\": [{\\\"id\\\": ext_id, \\\"enabled\\\": ext_info[\\\"enabled\\\"], \\\"capabilities\\\": ext_info[\\\"capabilities\\\"]} for ext_id, ext_info in EXTENSIONS.items()]}

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\", \\\"extensions\\\": list(EXTENSIONS.keys())}

if __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":
    import uvicorn
    logger.info(\\\"Starting R1 Extension Integration Service\\\")
    uvicorn.run(app, host=\\\"0.0.0.0\\\", port=8001)\" > extensions/extension_integration.py && sudo pkill -f \"uvicorn.*:8001\" || true && cd /llms/routing && source /novas/torch_venv/bin/activate && python3 extensions/extension_integration.py'"

class ExtensionRequest(BaseModel):

PROMPT_TEMPLATES = {
    \\\"browser\\\": \\\"Task: Browser Extension Operation\\\\nAgent: {agent_id}\\\\nAction: {action}\\\\nContext: {context}\\\\n\\\\nYou are an AI agent with browser capabilities. Execute the requested browser operation and provide a structured response.\\\",
    \\\"filesystem\\\": \\\"Task: Filesystem Operation\\\\nAgent: {agent_id}\\\\nAction: {action}\\\\nContext: {context}\\\\n\\\\nYou are an AI agent with filesystem capabilities. Execute the requested filesystem operation and provide a structured response.\\\",
    \\\"command\\\": \\\"Task: Command Execution\\\\nAgent: {agent_id}\\\\nAction: {action}\\\\nContext: {context}\\\\n\\\\nYou are an AI agent with command execution capabilities. Execute the requested command and provide a structured response.\\\",
    \\\"mcp\\\": \\\"Task: MCP Operation\\\\nAgent: {agent_id}\\\\nAction: {action}\\\\nContext: {context}\\\\n\\\\nYou are an AI agent with MCP capabilities. Execute the requested MCP operation and provide a structured response.\\\"
}

async def forward_to_r1(prompt: str, agent_id: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
    async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Forwarding request to R1: {prompt[:100]}...\\\")
            response = await client.post(
                \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\",
                json={
                    \\\"prompt\\\": prompt,
                    \\\"agent_id\\\": agent_id,
                    \\\"max_length\\\": max_length,
                    \\\"temperature\\\": temperature
                },
                timeout=30.0
            )
            response.raise_for_status()
            data = response.json()
            logger.info(f\\\"Got response from R1: {data}\\\")
            return data
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error forwarding to R1: {str(e)}\\\")
            if isinstance(e, httpx.HTTPError) and hasattr(e, \\\"response\\\"):
                logger.error(f\\\"Response content: {e.response.content}\\\")
            else:
                logger.error(\\\"No response content available\\\")
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.post(\\\"/extension/{extension_id}\\\")
async def handle_extension(extension_id: str, request: ExtensionRequest):
    if extension_id not in EXTENSIONS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} not found\\\")
    if not EXTENSIONS[extension_id][\\\"enabled\\\"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} is disabled\\\")
    try:
        prompt = PROMPT_TEMPLATES[extension_id].format(
            agent_id=request.agent_id,
            action=extension_id,
            context=request.prompt
        )
        logger.info(f\\\"Processing {extension_id} request from {request.agent_id}\\\")
        response = await forward_to_r1(
            prompt=prompt,
            agent_id=request.agent_id,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        return response
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error handling extension request: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.get(\\\"/extensions\\\")
async def list_extensions():
    return {\\\"extensions\\\": [{\\\"id\\\": ext_id, \\\"enabled\\\": ext_info[\\\"enabled\\\"], \\\"capabilities\\\": ext_info[\\\"capabilities\\\"]} for ext_id, ext_info in EXTENSIONS.items()]}

async def health_check():

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
import os
import json

import gc

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class GenerateRequest(BaseModel):
    prompt: str
    agent_id: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.device_ids = device_ids or list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.model = None
        self.tokenizer = None
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.current_device_idx = 0
    
    def load(self):
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")
            
            # Load tokenizer with local caching
            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True
            )
            
            # Configure device mapping
            device_map = \\\"auto\\\"
            logger.info(f\\\"Using automatic device map\\\")
            
            # Load model with optimizations
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                device_map=device_map,
                torch_dtype=torch.float16,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                low_cpu_mem_usage=True
            )
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
        try:
            # Get the device of the first parameter of the model
            device = next(self.model.parameters()).device
            logger.info(f\\\"Using device: {device}\\\")
            
            inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")
            # Move input tensors to the same device as the model
            inputs = {k: v.to(device) for k, v in inputs.items()}
            
            # Calculate max new tokens based on input length
            input_length = inputs[\\\"input_ids\\\"].shape[1]
            max_new_tokens = min(max_length - input_length, 512)  # Limit response length
            
            outputs = self.model.generate(
                **inputs,
                max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,
                temperature=temperature,
                do_sample=True,
                pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id,
                eos_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id,
                stop=[\\\"\\\\n\\\\n\\\", \\\"\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n\\\"]  # Stop on double or triple newlines
            )
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0][input_length:], skip_special_tokens=True)
            return response
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

# Initialize models dictionary
models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {}

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
    logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")
    
    # Initialize model
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    # Load models
    for name, model in models.items():
        if not model.load():
            logger.error(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")
            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")

@app.post(\\\"/generate\\\")
async def generate(request: GenerateRequest):
    try:
        model = models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"]
        response = model.generate(
            prompt=request.prompt,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        return {\\\"response\\\": response}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error handling request: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\", \\\"models\\\": list(models.keys())}

if __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":
    import uvicorn

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer, StoppingCriteria, StoppingCriteriaList
import os
import json
import logging
import psutil
import gc
import pynvml
from datetime import datetime

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

pynvml.nvmlInit()

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class GenerateRequest(BaseModel):
    prompt: str
    agent_id: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7

class DoubleNewlineStoppingCriteria(StoppingCriteria):
    def __init__(self, tokenizer):
        self.tokenizer = tokenizer
        self.double_newline = tokenizer.encode(\\\"\\\\n\\\\n\\\")[0]
        
    def __call__(self, input_ids: torch.LongTensor, scores: torch.FloatTensor, **kwargs) -> bool:
        last_token = input_ids[0][-1]
        return last_token == self.double_newline

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.device_ids = device_ids or list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.model = None
        self.tokenizer = None
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.current_device_idx = 0
    
    def load(self):
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")
            
            # Load tokenizer with local caching
            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True
            )
            
            # Configure device mapping
            device_map = \\\"auto\\\"
            logger.info(f\\\"Using automatic device map\\\")
            
            # Load model with optimizations
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                device_map=device_map,
                torch_dtype=torch.float16,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                low_cpu_mem_usage=True
            )
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
        try:
            # Get the device of the first parameter of the model
            device = next(self.model.parameters()).device
            logger.info(f\\\"Using device: {device}\\\")
            
            inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")
            # Move input tensors to the same device as the model
            inputs = {k: v.to(device) for k, v in inputs.items()}
            
            # Calculate max new tokens based on input length
            input_length = inputs[\\\"input_ids\\\"].shape[1]
            max_new_tokens = min(max_length - input_length, 512)  # Limit response length
            
            # Create stopping criteria
            stopping_criteria = StoppingCriteriaList([DoubleNewlineStoppingCriteria(self.tokenizer)])
            
            outputs = self.model.generate(
                **inputs,
                max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,
                temperature=temperature,
                do_sample=True,
                pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id,
                eos_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id,
                stopping_criteria=stopping_criteria
            )
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0][input_length:], skip_special_tokens=True)
            return response
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

# Initialize models dictionary
models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {}

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
    logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")
    
    # Initialize model
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    # Load models
    for name, model in models.items():
        if not model.load():
            logger.error(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")
            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")

@app.post(\\\"/generate\\\")
async def generate(request: GenerateRequest):
    try:
        model = models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"]
        response = model.generate(
            prompt=request.prompt,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        return {\\\"response\\\": response}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error handling request: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
