from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import httpx
import json
import logging
import os
from datetime import datetime

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"R1 Extension Integration\\\")

class ExtensionRequest(BaseModel):
    extension_id: str
    prompt: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    agent_id: str

EXTENSIONS = {
    \\\"browser\\\": {\\\"enabled\\\": True, \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\", \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"web_search\\\", \\\"screenshot\\\", \\\"interaction\\\"]},
    \\\"filesystem\\\": {\\\"enabled\\\": True, \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\", \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"read\\\", \\\"write\\\", \\\"search\\\", \\\"list\\\"]},
    \\\"command\\\": {\\\"enabled\\\": True, \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\", \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"execute\\\", \\\"monitor\\\", \\\"control\\\"]},
    \\\"mcp\\\": {\\\"enabled\\\": True, \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\", \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"tool_use\\\", \\\"resource_access\\\"]}
}

PROMPT_TEMPLATES = {
    \\\"browser\\\": \\\"You are an AI agent with browser capabilities. Please help with the following request:\\\\n\\\\n{context}\\\\n\\\\nProvide a clear and concise response.\\\",
    \\\"filesystem\\\": \\\"You are an AI agent with filesystem capabilities. Please help with the following request:\\\\n\\\\n{context}\\\\n\\\\nProvide a clear and concise response.\\\",
    \\\"command\\\": \\\"You are an AI agent with command execution capabilities. Please help with the following request:\\\\n\\\\n{context}\\\\n\\\\nProvide a clear and concise response, focusing on the code or command needed.\\\",
    \\\"mcp\\\": \\\"You are an AI agent with MCP capabilities. Please help with the following request:\\\\n\\\\n{context}\\\\n\\\\nProvide a clear and concise response.\\\"
}

async def forward_to_r1(prompt: str, agent_id: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=120.0) as client:
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Forwarding request to R1: {prompt[:100]}...\\\")
            response = await client.post(
                \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\",
                json={
                    \\\"prompt\\\": prompt,
                    \\\"agent_id\\\": agent_id,
                    \\\"max_length\\\": max_length,
                    \\\"temperature\\\": temperature
                }
            )
            response.raise_for_status()
            data = response.json()
            logger.info(f\\\"Got response from R1: {data}\\\")
            return data
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error forwarding to R1: {str(e)}\\\")
            if isinstance(e, httpx.HTTPError) and hasattr(e, \\\"response\\\"):
                logger.error(f\\\"Response content: {e.response.content}\\\")
            else:
                logger.error(\\\"No response content available\\\")
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.post(\\\"/extension/{extension_id}\\\")
async def handle_extension(extension_id: str, request: ExtensionRequest):
    if extension_id not in EXTENSIONS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} not found\\\")
    if not EXTENSIONS[extension_id][\\\"enabled\\\"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} is disabled\\\")
    try:
        prompt = PROMPT_TEMPLATES[extension_id].format(
            context=request.prompt
        )
        logger.info(f\\\"Processing {extension_id} request from {request.agent_id}\\\")
        response = await forward_to_r1(
            prompt=prompt,
            agent_id=request.agent_id,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        return response
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error handling extension request: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.get(\\\"/extensions\\\")
async def list_extensions():
    return {\\\"extensions\\\": [{\\\"id\\\": ext_id, \\\"enabled\\\": ext_info[\\\"enabled\\\"], \\\"capabilities\\\": ext_info[\\\"capabilities\\\"]} for ext_id, ext_info in EXTENSIONS.items()]}

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\", \\\"extensions\\\": list(EXTENSIONS.keys())}

if __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":
    import uvicorn
    logger.info(\\\"Starting R1 Extension Integration Service\\\")
    uvicorn.run(app, host=\\\"0.0.0.0\\\", port=8001)\" > extensions/extension_integration.py && sudo systemctl restart extension-router.service && sleep 5 && sudo journalctl -u extension-router.service -n 50 --no-pager'"

                local_files_only=True,
                low_cpu_mem_usage=True
            )

            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

            # Get the device of the first parameter of the model
            device = next(self.model.parameters()).device
            logger.info(f\\\"Using device: {device}\\\")

                do_sample=True,
                pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id,
                eos_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id,
                repetition_penalty=1.2,  # Penalize repetition
                no_repeat_ngram_size=3,  # Prevent repeating 3-grams
                num_beams=1,  # Use greedy decoding
                early_stopping=True  # Stop when EOS token is generated
            )
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0][input_length:], skip_special_tokens=True)
            return response
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")

        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} not found\\\")

        return response
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error handling extension request: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
import os
import json
import logging
import psutil
import gc
import pynvml
from datetime import datetime

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

pynvml.nvmlInit()

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class GenerateRequest(BaseModel):
    prompt: str
    agent_id: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.device_ids = device_ids or list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.model = None
        self.tokenizer = None
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.current_device_idx = 0
    
    def load(self):
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")
            
            # Load tokenizer with local caching
            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True
            )
            
            # Configure device mapping
            device_map = \\\"auto\\\"
            logger.info(f\\\"Using automatic device map\\\")
            
            # Load model with optimizations
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                device_map=device_map,
                torch_dtype=torch.float16,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                low_cpu_mem_usage=True
            )
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
        try:
            # Get the device of the first parameter of the model
            device = next(self.model.parameters()).device
            logger.info(f\\\"Using device: {device}\\\")
            
            inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")
            # Move input tensors to the same device as the model
            inputs = {k: v.to(device) for k, v in inputs.items()}
            
            # Calculate max new tokens based on input length
            input_length = inputs[\\\"input_ids\\\"].shape[1]
            max_new_tokens = min(max_length - input_length, 128)  # Limit response length
            
            outputs = self.model.generate(
                **inputs,
                max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,
                temperature=temperature,
                do_sample=True,
                pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id,
                eos_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id,
                repetition_penalty=1.5,  # Increase repetition penalty
                no_repeat_ngram_size=4,  # Prevent repeating 4-grams
                num_beams=1,  # Use greedy decoding
                early_stopping=True  # Stop when EOS token is generated
            )
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0][input_length:], skip_special_tokens=True)
            return response.strip()  # Strip whitespace
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

# Initialize models dictionary
models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {}

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
    logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")
    
    # Initialize model
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    # Load models
    for name, model in models.items():
        if not model.load():
            logger.error(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")
            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")

@app.post(\\\"/generate\\\")
async def generate(request: GenerateRequest):
    try:
        model = models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"]
        response = model.generate(
            prompt=request.prompt,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        return {\\\"response\\\": response}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error handling request: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\", \\\"models\\\": list(models.keys())}

if __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":
    import uvicorn
    uvicorn.run(app, host=\\\"0.0.0.0\\\", port=8000)\" > model_router.py && sudo systemctl restart model-router.service && sleep 5 && sudo journalctl -u model-router.service -n 50 --no-pager'"

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import httpx
import json
import logging
import os
from datetime import datetime

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"R1 Extension Integration\\\")

class ExtensionRequest(BaseModel):
    extension_id: str
    prompt: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    agent_id: str

EXTENSIONS = {
    \\\"browser\\\": {\\\"enabled\\\": True, \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\", \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"web_search\\\", \\\"screenshot\\\", \\\"interaction\\\"]},
    \\\"filesystem\\\": {\\\"enabled\\\": True, \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\", \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"read\\\", \\\"write\\\", \\\"search\\\", \\\"list\\\"]},
    \\\"command\\\": {\\\"enabled\\\": True, \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\", \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"execute\\\", \\\"monitor\\\", \\\"control\\\"]},
    \\\"mcp\\\": {\\\"enabled\\\": True, \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\", \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"tool_use\\\", \\\"resource_access\\\"]}
}

PROMPT_TEMPLATES = {
    \\\"browser\\\": \\\"You are an AI agent with browser capabilities. Please help with the following request:\\\\n\\\\n{context}\\\\n\\\\nProvide a clear and concise response.\\\",
    \\\"filesystem\\\": \\\"You are an AI agent with filesystem capabilities. Please help with the following request:\\\\n\\\\n{context}\\\\n\\\\nProvide a clear and concise response.\\\",
    \\\"command\\\": \\\"Write code for the following request. Provide ONLY the code, no explanations or additional text:\\\\n\\\\n{context}\\\",
    \\\"mcp\\\": \\\"You are an AI agent with MCP capabilities. Please help with the following request:\\\\n\\\\n{context}\\\\n\\\\nProvide a clear and concise response.\\\"
}

async def forward_to_r1(prompt: str, agent_id: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=120.0) as client:
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Forwarding request to R1: {prompt[:100]}...\\\")
            response = await client.post(
                \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\",
                json={
                    \\\"prompt\\\": prompt,
                    \\\"agent_id\\\": agent_id,
                    \\\"max_length\\\": max_length,
                    \\\"temperature\\\": temperature
                }
            )
            response.raise_for_status()
            data = response.json()
            logger.info(f\\\"Got response from R1: {data}\\\")
            return data
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error forwarding to R1: {str(e)}\\\")
            if isinstance(e, httpx.HTTPError) and hasattr(e, \\\"response\\\"):
                logger.error(f\\\"Response content: {e.response.content}\\\")
            else:
                logger.error(\\\"No response content available\\\")
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.post(\\\"/extension/{extension_id}\\\")
async def handle_extension(extension_id: str, request: ExtensionRequest):
    if extension_id not in EXTENSIONS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} not found\\\")
    if not EXTENSIONS[extension_id][\\\"enabled\\\"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} is disabled\\\")
    try:
        prompt = PROMPT_TEMPLATES[extension_id].format(
            context=request.prompt
        )
        logger.info(f\\\"Processing {extension_id} request from {request.agent_id}\\\")
        response = await forward_to_r1(
            prompt=prompt,
            agent_id=request.agent_id,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        return response
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error handling extension request: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.get(\\\"/extensions\\\")
async def list_extensions():
    return {\\\"extensions\\\": [{\\\"id\\\": ext_id, \\\"enabled\\\": ext_info[\\\"enabled\\\"], \\\"capabilities\\\": ext_info[\\\"capabilities\\\"]} for ext_id, ext_info in EXTENSIONS.items()]}

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\", \\\"extensions\\\": list(EXTENSIONS.keys())}

if __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":
    import uvicorn
    logger.info(\\\"Starting R1 Extension Integration Service\\\")

}

async def forward_to_r1(prompt: str, agent_id: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=120.0) as client:
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Forwarding request to R1: {prompt[:100]}...\\\")
            response = await client.post(
                \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\",
                json={
                    \\\"prompt\\\": prompt,
                    \\\"agent_id\\\": agent_id,
                    \\\"max_length\\\": max_length,
                    \\\"temperature\\\": temperature
                }
            )
            response.raise_for_status()
            data = response.json()
            logger.info(f\\\"Got response from R1: {data}\\\")
            return data
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error forwarding to R1: {str(e)}\\\")
            if isinstance(e, httpx.HTTPError) and hasattr(e, \\\"response\\\"):
                logger.error(f\\\"Response content: {e.response.content}\\\")
            else:
                logger.error(\\\"No response content available\\\")
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.post(\\\"/extension/{extension_id}\\\")
async def handle_extension(extension_id: str, request: ExtensionRequest):
    if extension_id not in EXTENSIONS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} not found\\\")
    if not EXTENSIONS[extension_id][\\\"enabled\\\"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} is disabled\\\")
    try:
        prompt = PROMPT_TEMPLATES[extension_id].format(
            context=request.prompt
        )
        logger.info(f\\\"Processing {extension_id} request from {request.agent_id}\\\")
        response = await forward_to_r1(
            prompt=prompt,
            agent_id=request.agent_id,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        return response
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error handling extension request: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.get(\\\"/extensions\\\")
async def list_extensions():
    return {\\\"extensions\\\": [{\\\"id\\\": ext_id, \\\"enabled\\\": ext_info[\\\"enabled\\\"], \\\"capabilities\\\": ext_info[\\\"capabilities\\\"]} for ext_id, ext_info in EXTENSIONS.items()]}

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\", \\\"extensions\\\": list(EXTENSIONS.keys())}

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
import os
import json
import logging
import psutil
import gc
import pynvml
from datetime import datetime

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

pynvml.nvmlInit()

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class GenerateRequest(BaseModel):
    prompt: str
    agent_id: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.device_ids = device_ids or list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.model = None
        self.tokenizer = None
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.current_device_idx = 0
    
    def load(self):
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")
            
            # Load tokenizer with local caching
            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True
            )
            
            # Configure device mapping
            device_map = \\\"auto\\\"
            logger.info(f\\\"Using automatic device map\\\")
            
            # Load model with optimizations
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                device_map=device_map,
                torch_dtype=torch.float16,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                low_cpu_mem_usage=True
            )
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
        try:
            # Get the device of the first parameter of the model
            device = next(self.model.parameters()).device
            logger.info(f\\\"Using device: {device}\\\")
            
            # Add system message
            system_message = \\\"You are a helpful AI assistant that writes Python code. When asked to write code, provide ONLY the exact code needed, with no explanations, comments, or additional text.\\\\n\\\\n\\\"
            full_prompt = system_message + prompt
            
            inputs = self.tokenizer(full_prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")
            # Move input tensors to the same device as the model
            inputs = {k: v.to(device) for k, v in inputs.items()}
            
            # Calculate max new tokens based on input length
            input_length = inputs[\\\"input_ids\\\"].shape[1]
            max_new_tokens = min(max_length - input_length, 64)  # Limit response length
            
            outputs = self.model.generate(
                **inputs,
                max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,
                temperature=temperature,
                do_sample=False,  # Use greedy decoding
                pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id,
                eos_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id,
                repetition_penalty=2.0,  # Increase repetition penalty
                no_repeat_ngram_size=4,  # Prevent repeating 4-grams
                num_beams=1,  # Use greedy decoding
                early_stopping=True  # Stop when EOS token is generated
            )
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0][input_length:], skip_special_tokens=True)
            return response.strip()  # Strip whitespace
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

# Initialize models dictionary
models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {}

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
    logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")
    
    # Initialize model
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    # Load models
    for name, model in models.items():
        if not model.load():
            logger.error(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")
            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")

@app.post(\\\"/generate\\\")
async def generate(request: GenerateRequest):
    try:
        model = models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"]
        response = model.generate(
            prompt=request.prompt,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        return {\\\"response\\\": response}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error handling request: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\", \\\"models\\\": list(models.keys())}

if __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":
    import uvicorn
    uvicorn.run(app, host=\\\"0.0.0.0\\\", port=8000)\" > model_router.py && sudo systemctl restart model-router.service && sleep 5 && sudo journalctl -u model-router.service -n 50 --no-pager'"

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class GenerateRequest(BaseModel):
    prompt: str
    agent_id: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.device_ids = device_ids or list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.model = None
        self.tokenizer = None
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.current_device_idx = 0
    
    def load(self):
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")
            
            # Load tokenizer with local caching
            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True
            )
            
            # Configure device mapping
            device_map = \\\"auto\\\"
            logger.info(f\\\"Using automatic device map\\\")
            
            # Load model with optimizations
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                device_map=device_map,
                torch_dtype=torch.float16,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                low_cpu_mem_usage=True
            )
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
        try:
            # Get the device of the first parameter of the model
            device = next(self.model.parameters()).device
            logger.info(f\\\"Using device: {device}\\\")
            
            # Add system message
            system_message = \\\"You are a helpful AI assistant that writes Python code. When asked to write code, provide ONLY the exact code needed, with no explanations, comments, or additional text. For example, if asked to write a hello world program, respond with: print(\\\\\\\"Hello World\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n\\\"
            full_prompt = system_message + prompt
            
            inputs = self.tokenizer(full_prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")
            # Move input tensors to the same device as the model
            inputs = {k: v.to(device) for k, v in inputs.items()}
            
            # Calculate max new tokens based on input length
            input_length = inputs[\\\"input_ids\\\"].shape[1]
            max_new_tokens = min(max_length - input_length, 32)  # Limit response length
            
            outputs = self.model.generate(
                **inputs,
                max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,
                temperature=temperature,
                do_sample=False,  # Use greedy decoding
                pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id,
                eos_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id,
                repetition_penalty=2.0,  # Increase repetition penalty
                no_repeat_ngram_size=4,  # Prevent repeating 4-grams
                num_beams=1,  # Use greedy decoding
                early_stopping=True  # Stop when EOS token is generated
            )
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0][input_length:], skip_special_tokens=True)
            return response.strip()  # Strip whitespace
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

# Initialize models dictionary
models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {}

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
    logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")
    
    # Initialize model
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    # Load models
    for name, model in models.items():
        if not model.load():
            logger.error(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")
            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")

@app.post(\\\"/generate\\\")
async def generate(request: GenerateRequest):
    try:
        model = models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"]
        response = model.generate(
            prompt=request.prompt,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        return {\\\"response\\\": response}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error handling request: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\", \\\"models\\\": list(models.keys())}

if __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":
    import uvicorn
    uvicorn.run(app, host=\\\"0.0.0.0\\\", port=8000)\" > model_router.py && sudo systemctl restart model-router.service && sleep 5 && sudo journalctl -u model-router.service -n 50 --no-pager'"

from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union
import httpx
import json
import logging
import os
from datetime import datetime

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"R1 Extension Integration\\\")

class ExtensionRequest(BaseModel):
    extension_id: str
    prompt: str
    max_length: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    agent_id: str

EXTENSIONS = {
    \\\"browser\\\": {\\\"enabled\\\": True, \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\", \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"web_search\\\", \\\"screenshot\\\", \\\"interaction\\\"]},
    \\\"filesystem\\\": {\\\"enabled\\\": True, \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\", \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"read\\\", \\\"write\\\", \\\"search\\\", \\\"list\\\"]},
    \\\"command\\\": {\\\"enabled\\\": True, \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\", \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"execute\\\", \\\"monitor\\\", \\\"control\\\"]},
    \\\"mcp\\\": {\\\"enabled\\\": True, \\\"endpoint\\\": \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\", \\\"capabilities\\\": [\\\"tool_use\\\", \\\"resource_access\\\"]}
}

PROMPT_TEMPLATES = {
    \\\"browser\\\": \\\"You are an AI agent with browser capabilities. Please help with the following request:\\\\n\\\\n{context}\\\\n\\\\nProvide a clear and concise response.\\\",
    \\\"filesystem\\\": \\\"You are an AI agent with filesystem capabilities. Please help with the following request:\\\\n\\\\n{context}\\\\n\\\\nProvide a clear and concise response.\\\",
    \\\"command\\\": \\\"Write Python code for: {context}\\\\nProvide ONLY the exact code, no explanations or additional text.\\\",
    \\\"mcp\\\": \\\"You are an AI agent with MCP capabilities. Please help with the following request:\\\\n\\\\n{context}\\\\n\\\\nProvide a clear and concise response.\\\"
}

async def forward_to_r1(prompt: str, agent_id: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=120.0) as client:
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Forwarding request to R1: {prompt[:100]}...\\\")
            response = await client.post(
                \\\"http://localhost:8000/generate\\\",
                json={
                    \\\"prompt\\\": prompt,
                    \\\"agent_id\\\": agent_id,
                    \\\"max_length\\\": max_length,
                    \\\"temperature\\\": temperature
                }
            )
            response.raise_for_status()
            data = response.json()
            logger.info(f\\\"Got response from R1: {data}\\\")
            return data
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error forwarding to R1: {str(e)}\\\")
            if isinstance(e, httpx.HTTPError) and hasattr(e, \\\"response\\\"):
                logger.error(f\\\"Response content: {e.response.content}\\\")
            else:
                logger.error(\\\"No response content available\\\")
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.post(\\\"/extension/{extension_id}\\\")
async def handle_extension(extension_id: str, request: ExtensionRequest):
    if extension_id not in EXTENSIONS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} not found\\\")
    if not EXTENSIONS[extension_id][\\\"enabled\\\"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=f\\\"Extension {extension_id} is disabled\\\")
    try:
        prompt = PROMPT_TEMPLATES[extension_id].format(
            context=request.prompt
        )
        logger.info(f\\\"Processing {extension_id} request from {request.agent_id}\\\")
        response = await forward_to_r1(
            prompt=prompt,
            agent_id=request.agent_id,
            max_length=request.max_length,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        return response
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error handling extension request: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.get(\\\"/extensions\\\")
async def list_extensions():
    return {\\\"extensions\\\": [{\\\"id\\\": ext_id, \\\"enabled\\\": ext_info[\\\"enabled\\\"], \\\"capabilities\\\": ext_info[\\\"capabilities\\\"]} for ext_id, ext_info in EXTENSIONS.items()]}

@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")
async def health_check():
    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\", \\\"extensions\\\": list(EXTENSIONS.keys())}

if __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":
    import uvicorn
    logger.info(\\\"Starting R1 Extension Integration Service\\\")
    uvicorn.run(app, host=\\\"0.0.0.0\\\", port=8001)\" > extensions/extension_integration.py && sudo systemctl restart extension-router.service && sleep 5 && sudo journalctl -u extension-router.service -n 50 --no-pager'"

        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    # Load models
    for name, model in models.items():
        if not model.load():
            logger.error(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")
            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")

@app.post(\\\"/generate\\\")
async def generate(request: GenerateRequest):

        model = models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"]
        response = model.generate(
            prompt=request.prompt,
            max_length=request.max_length,

        )
        return {\\\"response\\\": response}
    except Exception as e:

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union, Any
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
import os
import json
import logging
import psutil
import gc
import pynvml
from datetime import datetime

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

pynvml.nvmlInit()

app = FastAPI(title=\\\"Model Router\\\")

class CompletionRequest(BaseModel):
    model: str
    prompt: str
    max_tokens: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    stream: Optional[bool] = False
    stop: Optional[List[str]] = None

class ChatMessage(BaseModel):
    role: str
    content: str

class ChatCompletionRequest(BaseModel):
    model: str
    messages: List[ChatMessage]
    max_tokens: Optional[int] = 2048
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7
    stream: Optional[bool] = False
    stop: Optional[List[str]] = None

class ModelConfig:
    def __init__(self, model_id: str, model_path: str, device_ids: List[int] = None, max_length: int = 2048):
        self.model_id = model_id
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.device_ids = device_ids or list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.model = None
        self.tokenizer = None
        self.last_used = None
        self.total_requests = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.current_device_idx = 0
    
    def load(self):
        try:
            logger.info(f\\\"Loading model {self.model_id} from {self.model_path}\\\")
            
            # Load tokenizer with local caching
            self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True
            )
            
            # Configure device mapping
            device_map = \\\"auto\\\"
            logger.info(f\\\"Using automatic device map\\\")
            
            # Load model with optimizations
            self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
                self.model_path,
                device_map=device_map,
                torch_dtype=torch.float16,
                trust_remote_code=True,
                local_files_only=True,
                low_cpu_mem_usage=True
            )
            
            self.last_used = datetime.now()
            logger.info(\\\"Model loaded successfully\\\")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error loading model: {str(e)}\\\")
            return False

    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_length: int = 2048, temperature: float = 0.7):
        try:
            # Get the device of the first parameter of the model
            device = next(self.model.parameters()).device
            logger.info(f\\\"Using device: {device}\\\")
            
            inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")
            # Move input tensors to the same device as the model
            inputs = {k: v.to(device) for k, v in inputs.items()}
            
            # Calculate max new tokens based on input length
            input_length = inputs[\\\"input_ids\\\"].shape[1]
            max_new_tokens = min(max_length - input_length, 64)  # Limit response length
            
            outputs = self.model.generate(
                **inputs,
                max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,
                temperature=temperature,
                do_sample=False,  # Use greedy decoding
                pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id,
                eos_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id,
                repetition_penalty=2.0,  # Increase repetition penalty
                no_repeat_ngram_size=4,  # Prevent repeating 4-grams
                num_beams=1,  # Use greedy decoding
                early_stopping=True  # Stop when EOS token is generated
            )
            response = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0][input_length:], skip_special_tokens=True)
            return response.strip()  # Strip whitespace
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f\\\"Error generating response: {str(e)}\\\")
            raise

# Initialize models dictionary
models: Dict[str, ModelConfig] = {}

MODEL_MAP = {
    \\\"gpt-3.5-turbo\\\": \\\"deepseek-r1\\\",  # Map OpenAI model names to our models
    \\\"gpt-4\\\": \\\"deepseek-r1\\\",
    \\\"text-davinci-003\\\": \\\"deepseek-r1\\\"
}

async def verify_api_key(api_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias=\\\"Authorization\\\")):
    if not api_key:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail=\\\"API key required\\\")
    if not api_key.startswith(\\\"Bearer \\\"):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail=\\\"Invalid API key format\\\")
    # Add your API key validation logic here
    return api_key

@app.on_event(\\\"startup\\\")
async def startup_event():
    logger.info(\\\"Starting model router service\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU count: {torch.cuda.device_count()}\\\")
    logger.info(f\\\"GPU devices: {[torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i in range(torch.cuda.device_count())]}\\\")
    
    # Initialize model
    models[\\\"deepseek-r1\\\"] = ModelConfig(
        model_id=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        model_path=\\\"/llms/models/DeepSeek-R1\\\",
        device_ids=[0, 1],
        max_length=2048
    )
    
    # Load models
    for name, model in models.items():
        if not model.load():
            logger.error(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")
            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Failed to load model {name}\\\")

@app.post(\\\"/v1/completions\\\")
async def create_completion(request: CompletionRequest, api_key: str = Depends(verify_api_key)):
    try:
        model_name = MODEL_MAP.get(request.model, request.model)
        if model_name not in models:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f\\\"Model {request.model} not found\\\")
        
        model = models[model_name]
        response = model.generate(
            prompt=request.prompt,
            max_length=request.max_tokens,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        
        return {
            \\\"id\\\": f\\\"cmpl-{datetime.now().timestamp()}\\\",
            \\\"object\\\": \\\"text_completion\\\",
            \\\"created\\\": int(datetime.now().timestamp()),
            \\\"model\\\": request.model,
            \\\"choices\\\": [{
                \\\"text\\\": response,
                \\\"index\\\": 0,
                \\\"logprobs\\\": None,
                \\\"finish_reason\\\": \\\"stop\\\"
            }],
            \\\"usage\\\": {
                \\\"prompt_tokens\\\": 0,  # Add token counting
                \\\"completion_tokens\\\": 0,
                \\\"total_tokens\\\": 0
            }
        }
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error handling request: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.post(\\\"/v1/chat/completions\\\")
async def create_chat_completion(request: ChatCompletionRequest, api_key: str = Depends(verify_api_key)):
    try:
        model_name = MODEL_MAP.get(request.model, request.model)
        if model_name not in models:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f\\\"Model {request.model} not found\\\")
        
        # Format messages into a prompt
        prompt = \\\"\\\"
        for msg in request.messages:
            if msg.role == \\\"system\\\":
                prompt += f\\\"System: {msg.content}\\\\n\\\"
            elif msg.role == \\\"user\\\":
                prompt += f\\\"User: {msg.content}\\\\n\\\"
            elif msg.role == \\\"assistant\\\":
                prompt += f\\\"Assistant: {msg.content}\\\\n\\\"
        prompt += \\\"Assistant: \\\"
        
        model = models[model_name]
        response = model.generate(
            prompt=prompt,
            max_length=request.max_tokens,
            temperature=request.temperature
        )
        
        return {
            \\\"id\\\": f\\\"chatcmpl-{datetime.now().timestamp()}\\\",
            \\\"object\\\": \\\"chat.completion\\\",
            \\\"created\\\": int(datetime.now().timestamp()),
            \\\"model\\\": request.model,
            \\\"choices\\\": [{
                \\\"index\\\": 0,
                \\\"message\\\": {
                    \\\"role\\\": \\\"assistant\\\",
                    \\\"content\\\": response
                },
                \\\"finish_reason\\\": \\\"stop\\\"
            }],
            \\\"usage\\\": {
                \\\"prompt_tokens\\\": 0,  # Add token counting
                \\\"completion_tokens\\\": 0,
                \\\"total_tokens\\\": 0
            }
        }
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f\\\"Error handling request: {str(e)}\\\")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.get(\\\"/v1/models\\\")

            for model_id in MODEL_MAP.keys()
        ],
        \\\"object\\\": \\\"list\\\"
    }

    print("DNS record created successfully!")

    print(f"Error creating DNS record: {response.status_code}")

I see the DNS record already exists, and there are a few issues with the page rule. Let me modify the script to update the existing DNS record and fix the page rule:


Roo wants to execute this command:
echo 'import requests
import os

# Cloudflare API configuration
CF_API_TOKEN = "_rJG86h3aGVXfDdyACLezQ6Z04GRZB8S5AxC45hd"
CF_ZONE_ID = "5f446859d70104292a3d42a3fc65a680"
CF_EMAIL = "chase@levelup2x.com"

# API endpoint
CF_API_URL = f"https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/zones/{CF_ZONE_ID}/dns_records"

# Headers for authentication
headers = {
    "Authorization": f"Bearer {CF_API_TOKEN}",
    "Content-Type": "application/json"
}

# First, list existing DNS records to find the record ID
response = requests.get(CF_API_URL, headers=headers)
records = response.json()

record_id = None
for record in records.get("result", []):
    if record["name"] == "llm.a-d-a-p-t.ai":
        record_id = record["id"]
        break

# DNS record configuration
dns_record = {
    "type": "A",
    "name": "llm.a-d-a-p-t.ai",
    "content": "34.132.46.49",  # Your GCP VM IP
    "ttl": 120,  # Minimum TTL allowed
    "proxied": True  # Enable Cloudflare proxy
}

if record_id:
    # Update existing record
    update_url = f"{CF_API_URL}/{record_id}"
    response = requests.put(update_url, headers=headers, json=dns_record)
    
    if response.status_code == 200:
        print("DNS record updated successfully!")
        print(response.json())
    else:
        print(f"Error updating DNS record: {response.status_code}")
        print(response.text)
else:
    print("DNS record not found")

# Create Cloudflare Page Rule for SSL
page_rules_url = f"https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/zones/{CF_ZONE_ID}/pagerules"

page_rule = {
    "targets": [
        {
            "target": "url",
            "constraint": {
                "operator": "matches",
                "value": "llm.a-d-a-p-t.ai/*"
            }
        }
    ],
    "actions": [
        {
            "id": "ssl",
            "value": "strict"
        },
        {
            "id": "browser_cache_ttl",
            "value": 120  # Minimum TTL allowed
        }
    ],
    "status": "active",
    "priority": 1
}

# First, list existing page rules
response = requests.get(page_rules_url, headers=headers)
rules = response.json()

rule_id = None
for rule in rules.get("result", []):
    if any(target["constraint"]["value"] == "llm.a-d-a-p-t.ai/*" for target in rule["targets"]):
        rule_id = rule["id"]
        break

if rule_id:
    # Update existing rule
    update_url = f"{page_rules_url}/{rule_id}"
    response = requests.put(update_url, headers=headers, json=page_rule)
else:
    # Create new rule
    response = requests.post(page_rules_url, headers=headers, json=page_rule)

if response.status_code == 200:
    print("\nPage rule created/updated successfully!")
    print(response.json())
else:
    print(f"\nError with page rule: {response.status_code}")
    print(response.text)' > setup_cloudflare.py && python3 setup_cloudflare.py
